# roll code — test coverage

Every feature of phases 1–3 maps to the tests that cover it: every route and the statuses it
answers, the auth matrix, every event, MCP tool and CLI command, every GitHub event, every merge
queue transition, every review rule and blocker, and every mode, command, error and job state of
roll's reviewer. `pnpm test:code` runs them (about 640 Rust tests across the code crates and `roll code`,
side by side in about 20 s warm, and about 630 vitest tests for the dashboard); `--docker` adds Postgres,
real Docker previews and the cluster of real `rolld` containers, `--stress` the throughput runs.

GitHub Actions uses `.github/workflows/ci.yml` for pull requests and merge groups. Its merge
gate finishes in under a minute: Rust smoke tests, TypeScript checks (TypeScript 7), the dashboard's
vitest suite in three shards, its node tests, the desktop app's tests, and script/workflow validation,
each job in parallel with a two-minute timeout. It does not run the full feature map before merging.
The full code suite remains available manually in `.github/workflows/code.yml`; the other
service workflows are also manual. Docker matrices, conformance and stress tests run there.

`crates/code-control/tests/coverage_map.rs` keeps this map honest. It fails when a route the
routers declare, an MCP tool or an event kind has no row here naming a test, or when a test named
here doesn't exist. `roll-cli`'s `every_code_command_is_in_the_coverage_map` does the same for
every `roll code` command. When you add a feature, add its row.

Paths are relative to `crates/`, except the dashboard rows. `file::fn` names a test function; a
bare `file.rs` without a crate is code-control's `tests/` (or the crate the section is about).
## HTTP routes

Every method and path the routers declare (`api.rs`, `review/api.rs`, `ai/api.rs`, `mcp.rs` and
code-git's `/git/{*path}`), with every status it answers short of a 500. Errors are JSON `{error}`
everywhere, bad query strings included. 401 means no or a bad token, 403 a caller who can see the
thing but not do it, 404 one who can't see it (docs/code/http-api.md).

#### Sandboxes (`agents/sandbox/`)

| Behaviour | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| the reconciler creates the agent37 instance from roll's template with the size, `auto_sleep: false`, `metadata.roll_sandbox` and env (the machine token, `ROLL_API_URL`, the owner's provider keys); the sealed secrets are dropped once created; the token opens the relay link as the sandbox's machine; `Machine.sandbox` follows the status | `sandboxes.rs::a_sandbox_runs_on_agent37_from_create_to_delete` |
| creates are retried on capacity errors, a create whose answer was lost is adopted by roll's id (never made twice), a refused create fails with agent37's reason and isn't retried; always on (an instance that stopped is started again); an instance agent37 lost fails the sandbox; removing a sandbox's machine deletes the sandbox | `sandboxes.rs::creates_are_retried_adopted_or_failed_and_limits_hold` |
| running time is metered per UTC month (split at month boundaries) and priced from the size's hourly price (one hour of 3¢ is $0.03, 90 minutes $0.045) | `sandboxes.rs::a_sandbox_runs_on_agent37_from_create_to_delete`, `code-control/src/agents/sandbox/mod.rs::months_split_at_their_boundaries` |
| the agent37 client: a refused key is an error that names the variable, not the key; answers are bounded; unreachable is retryable; statuses and errors map to retry or not; ids are checked before they go in a path | `sandboxes.rs::the_agent37_client_is_bounded_and_keeps_its_key`, `code-control/src/agents/sandbox/agent37.rs::statuses_map_to_roll_states`, `code-control/src/agents/sandbox/agent37.rs::errors_say_whether_to_retry`, `code-control/src/agents/sandbox/agent37.rs::ids_are_checked_before_they_go_in_a_path` |
| the fargate backend (fake ECS, S3 in process or MinIO): a sandbox is a task of the sandbox template named after it (a create whose answer was lost finds it), its env sealed in S3 and handed over in a bundle (never in `RunTask`, never in the clear in the bucket); asleep is no task, waking starts a new one with the same env; delete stops it and drops what was kept; `roll-sbx-<id>` ↔ `sbx_<id>` | `code-preview/tests/fargate.rs::sandboxes_are_tasks_that_start_again_with_their_sealed_env`, `code-preview/src/sandbox.rs::names_map_to_sandboxes` |
| the docker backend (real Docker; skipped without it): no new privileges, all capabilities dropped, pids/memory (no swap)/CPU limits, an init, a named home volume, no host network or Docker socket, env through a file that's removed; idempotent create, stop, start, delete with the volume | `sandboxes.rs::the_docker_backend_runs_hardened_containers`, `code-control/src/agents/sandbox/docker.rs::run_args_are_hardened_and_carry_no_secrets` |
| the sandbox image (`deploy/agent/Dockerfile`; opt-in with `ROLL_SANDBOX_TEST_IMAGE`): as `roll` without capabilities, `roll agent up` takes the token from its env and comes online with codex and claude installed | `sandboxes.rs::the_sandbox_image_comes_online_with_its_token` |
| config from the environment (backend, sizes, limit), the agent37 key never in `Debug`; an owner's env is validated (names, reserved `ROLL_*`/`AGENT37_*`, sizes, one line) and the spec's `Debug` shows only names | `code-control/src/agents/sandbox/mod.rs::sizes_and_env_config`, `code-control/src/agents/sandbox/api.rs::env_is_checked` |
| sleep: a sandbox stays up while a thread on it runs, sleeps a window after it only waits on its user (stopped at agent37, `desired: asleep`, `stopped_reason: idle` on the sandbox and its machine), metering ends at the stop, its records still show (the thread `waiting`); `ROLL_SANDBOX_IDLE_STOP` and `ROLL_SANDBOX_WAKE_TIMEOUT` from the environment (`0` keeps sandboxes up) | `sandboxes.rs::an_idle_sandbox_sleeps_and_a_command_wakes_it`, `code-control/src/agents/sandbox/mod.rs::sizes_and_env_config` |
| terminal output keeps a sandbox up; silence puts it to sleep | `sandboxes.rs::an_idle_sandbox_sleeps_and_a_command_wakes_it` |
| a relay that starts (a restart, a lease moving) never puts a sandbox to sleep sooner than one window after it started, however old its last activity | `sandboxes.rs::a_restarted_relay_waits_a_whole_window_before_a_sandbox_sleeps` |
| wake: a command to an asleep sandbox sets it running (`starting`, at once), is held until the daemon connects again and is delivered exactly once; the wake counts as activity | `sandboxes.rs::an_idle_sandbox_sleeps_and_a_command_wakes_it` |
| a command to an owner-stopped sandbox starts it (`stopped_reason: owner` until then); a wake that runs out answers `machine_offline` "still waking" and the same id goes through once it's up; a sandbox being deleted never wakes (no start), a deleted one's machine is gone (404) | `sandboxes.rs::waking_times_out_clearly_and_deleted_sandboxes_never_wake` |

### Agents in roll code (`agents/code.rs`, docs/code/agents.md "Agents in roll code")

| Behaviour | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| grants: minted per command for the machine owner's repo (`project.add` clone: fetch; `thread.publish`: push, needs write), sent in the command frame; git for that repo only, as `agent:<machine>`; 401 on every API route; other owners' repos 400, missing ones 404; expired and revoked grants 401; commands without a repo get none | `routes.rs::agent_grants_follow_the_auth_matrix`, `mcp.rs::agent_tools_work_in_roll_code_like_their_routes` |
| `project.add` with `clone`: a real daemon clones into `<clone dir>/<owner>/<repo>` (or a relative path under it, `..` refused); the remote and `.git/config` hold no credentials; the project records `repo`; cloning again keeps the checkout | `agents_code.rs::repos_clone_onto_machines_and_new_repos_start_as_projects` |
| a new repo started on a machine as a project in one step (visibility, description), starting with a README commit so a thread can work in a worktree and publish at once, a retry answers the same, a bad name 422 | `agents_code.rs::repos_clone_onto_machines_and_new_repos_start_as_projects` |
| `thread.publish` in a roll code project: the branch becomes a change whose head is the commit (`Published.change`, `url`), publishing again revises it; `land` goes through the merge queue (`queued`), and is `blocked` with its blockers while required checks haven't passed | `agents_code.rs::published_threads_become_changes_that_land_through_the_queue` |
| `Thread.change` follows the change on the stream (blocked → checking → failing → ready → landed) without a `/state` load; the daemon's own updates of the thread keep it; once landed the relay sends `thread.change_ended` and the daemon settles the thread (`landed`) | `agents_code.rs::published_threads_become_changes_that_land_through_the_queue` |
| `Thread.work`: claimed for the thread (as `agent:<machine>`), 204 when none is left, let go, a given `owner/repo#N` taken; kept across the daemon's updates | `agents_code.rs::publishing_does_the_threads_work_item` |
| a thread's claim is kept alive (renewed past its lease) while the thread runs; it runs out like any other once the thread sits idle past the grace, or its machine goes offline | `agents_code.rs::a_working_threads_claim_stays_alive_and_lapses_when_it_stops` |
| `thread.publish` links the change to the thread's work item (`PATCH /v1/code/changes/{id}` `work_item`): the item goes to `review`, lists the change, and is `done` once the change lands through the queue; another item, an unknown one, a non-string or a landed change refused | `agents_code.rs::publishing_does_the_threads_work_item` |
| `thread.publish` while roll can't be reached, in a GitHub-connected repo (the grant names its GitHub side): the thread's branch goes to GitHub and a pull request opens with the user's own token, the reply's landing is `queued`, the default branch is never pushed | `agent-host/tests/threads.rs::publish_falls_back_to_github_while_roll_is_down` |
| the first publish of an empty repo (a thread in the project folder, no worktree possible): the grant names the default branch while it's absent, the folder's files become its first commit with the same id (it lands as pushed), a clone that's too late gets a 400 ("has commits now"), a repo that requires reviews gets it as a change on `roll/first-commit` (blocked until reviewed) the thread follows | `agents_code.rs::the_first_publish_of_an_empty_repo_starts_its_default_branch` |
| auto-pull of a roll code project: turning it on (`project.update`, with a fetch grant) pulls at once; a change landing on the default branch is pulled by itself (the relay sends `project.pull` with a fetch-only grant; the grant is never written down); a checkout with local changes is skipped and left as it is; `project.pull` by hand answers `pulled`; agent tokens can't change settings or pull (403) | `agents_code.rs::roll_code_projects_with_auto_pull_follow_their_default_branch`, `code-control/src/agents/policy.rs::agents_drive_threads_but_never_reach_past_them` |

| Slack's and Discord's list calls: `conversations.list` (public and private, not archived, ≤ 200, the cursor), `users.list`, `usergroups.list`, a guild's channels, roles and members (search and list, ≤ 1000, `after`), one member (absent: none); bearer and bot tokens; answers read bounded | `code-chat/tests/client.rs::slack_lists_page_with_cursors_and_bounded_limits`, `code-chat/tests/client.rs::discord_lists_a_guilds_channels_roles_and_members` |
| chat ops' catalog entries (3 triggers, 13 steps with their JSON Schemas and samples; every field that names a workspace, server, channel, person or a channel-or-person a `format: "lookup"` picker, threads and machines roll's own) are what the dashboard's demo and template tests use (`apps/dashboard/src/lib/chatops-catalog.json`, written from the registry); every chat ops template builds a spec the builder's checks accept | `chatops.rs::the_dashboard_has_chat_ops_catalog`, `apps/dashboard/src/lib/automation-templates.test.ts` |
| GitHub steps: a comment once per run and step, labels (a missing one fine), commit statuses, a pull request from a roll change (its head pushed, the change linked, once), only the owner's own connected repos | `github.rs::automation_steps_comment_label_status_and_open_a_pull_request` |

| Behaviour | Tests |

| rename: 301 with `{redirect}` and `Location`, the name can be reused, survives a restart | `api.rs::repos_rename_and_visibility`, `contract.rs::state_survives_a_restart` |
| refs, tree (kinds, modes, sizes, `last_change`), blob (content types, CSP, `nosniff`, ETag, immutable caching by full id), paths (cap, `truncated`), log (cursor, path), changeset by id, prefix or git commit, compare | `api.rs::land_merge_and_conflict`, `contract.rs::writes_binary_content_deletes_and_blob_caching`, `contract.rs::paths_are_capped` |
| readme: rendering, sanitizing, relative links and images (markdown and raw HTML, `srcset`), README in a subdirectory | `api.rs::readme_is_rendered_and_sanitized`, `code-control/src/render.rs` tests |
| `POST /changesets`, then land `{changeset}`; `content_b64`, `delete`, modes | `contract.rs::writes_binary_content_deletes_and_blob_caching` |
| `POST /land`: fast-forward, rebase, server-side merge, 409 conflict body, `ref` | `api.rs::land_merge_and_conflict` |
| changes: create, revise (checks reset), land with required checks (queued, 202), conflict, close, by number, `#n` or id, author attribution | `api.rs::changes_and_work`, `contract.rs::change_authors`, `contract.rs::lookups_by_number_and_id` |
| changes: `draft` (create, `PATCH`, blocks landing on both policies, dequeues), `PATCH` title/body validation, `approve-run` (per head, audited) | `queue.rs::blockers_keep_changes_out_of_the_queue` |
| checks: external report, status validation, timestamps | `api.rs::changes_and_work`, `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks`, `contract.rs::errors_are_json_with_the_right_status` |
| previews: create and delete through a backend; 422 without one | `contract.rs::previews_use_pooled_work_trees`, `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks`, `contract.rs::errors_are_json_with_the_right_status` |
| work: create, priority and label validation, claim (priority order, labels, 204), lease expiry, heartbeat (keeps the lease length), release, complete, patch, someone else's lease (409) | `api.rs::changes_and_work`, `contract.rs::lookups_by_number_and_id`, `contract.rs::errors_are_json_with_the_right_status` |
| SSE: `landed`, `change`, `work`, `check`, `preview` shapes, per-repo isolation, private feeds | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks`, `routes.rs::every_repo_route_follows_the_auth_matrix` |
| errors: JSON `{error}` with the right status for bad JSON, unknown revs, bad paths, bad enums and every 404 | `contract.rs::errors_are_json_with_the_right_status` |
| GitHub endpoints without an App: info, installations, setup redirect, webhook 404, connect refused | `contract.rs::open_mode_makes_everyone_admin`, `contract.rs::auth_matrix`, `routes.rs::bad_tokens_are_401_on_every_route` |
| restart: owners, repos, redirects, changes, checks, work items, the number counter | `contract.rs::state_survives_a_restart` |
| blame: per-line commits equal `git blame --first-parent` of a pushed history with a merge; author/time/message per range; cached; a revision is echoed; 404 file and revision | `webhooks.rs::blame_matches_git` |
| search at a revision (`rev`): the change's own edits and deletions, the index for the rest, `overlay`, resolved `rev`, globs and limits on both halves, 404 revision, 400 bad regex | `webhooks.rs::search_at_a_change_head`, `mcp.rs::tools_map_onto_their_routes` |
| bad query strings answer a JSON 400 (core and reviewer routes) | `routes.rs::statuses_of_every_route`, `reviewer.rs::repo_reviewer_routes_answer_every_status` |

## Auth matrix

Every cell is asserted for the API, git and MCP. API: `contract.rs::auth_matrix` and, for all 55
repo, change and work routes, `routes.rs::every_repo_route_follows_the_auth_matrix`; git:
`git_more.rs::git_auth_matrix_over_smart_http` (plus `git_more.rs::git_auth_for_other_owners_and_anonymous`,
`tokens.rs::tokens_are_listed_and_revoked_everywhere_at_once`); MCP: `mcp.rs::every_tool_is_authorized_like_its_route`
(plus `mcp.rs::invalid_tokens_and_foreign_sessions`).
Open mode: `contract.rs::open_mode_makes_everyone_admin`.

| Caller | Read public | Read private | Write public | Write private |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| open mode (no `ROLL_CODE_TOKEN` or `ROLL_ADMIN_TOKEN`) | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ |
| admin token (`ROLL_CODE_TOKEN`, or rolld's operator token: `rolld/tests/auth.rs::every_control_plane_route_needs_the_operator_token`) | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ |
| owner's token | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ |
| another owner's token | ✓ | 404 | 403 | 404 |
| anonymous | ✓ | 404 (git: 401 with a Basic challenge, so git sends credentials) | 401 | 404 (git: 401) |
| unknown, garbage or revoked token | 401 | 401 | 401 | 401 |

- Unknown, garbage, revoked and unknown `rla_` tokens get 401 on every route that looks at the
  caller: `routes.rs::bad_tokens_are_401_on_every_route`, `tokens.rs::tokens_are_listed_and_revoked_everywhere_at_once`.
- Token listing and revocation (owner or admin only; 404/401/403 for others; revoked at once for
  the API, git and MCP): `tokens.rs::tokens_are_listed_and_revoked_everywhere_at_once`.
- Agent tokens (`agent:<name>`) land code and read private repos, but get 403 on every management
  route (tokens, owner, repo settings and delete, review rules, reviewer, webhooks, queue
  pause/resume, approve-run, billing, provider, review apps) and 404 on the owner-only reads. They
  review only as their own agent: `tokens.rs::agent_tokens_work_code_but_cant_manage_or_impersonate`,
  `routes.rs::every_repo_route_follows_the_auth_matrix`.
- Review-app tokens read the repos the app is installed on (private ones too) and get 403 on every
  write and management route there, 404 elsewhere; owner and admin routes refuse them; they can
  approve or request changes only where installed: `routes.rs::every_repo_route_follows_the_auth_matrix`,
  `review.rs::app_tokens_are_scoped_even_on_an_open_daemon`,
  `review.rs::review_apps_events_scopes_requests_and_failed_deliveries`.
- A signed-in user's session (docs/accounts.md, rolld's gate) is their own user owner, made on
  first sign-in; an admin's is the admin: `rolld/tests/auth.rs::every_control_plane_route_needs_the_operator_token`,
  `roll-accounts/tests/flows.rs::an_invite_becomes_a_session_by_email_code`.
- Anonymous writes are 401 (the dashboard opens its sign-in prompt), never 403, including owner
  create, owner patch and token create: `api.rs::repos_rename_and_visibility`, `routes.rs::every_repo_route_follows_the_auth_matrix`.

## Events

Every kind `Code::emit` sends is in `webhooks::EVENTS`, and
`webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks` produces each one and checks
that SSE and the webhook delivery carry the same data, with exact key sets, inside the delivery
envelope `{event, delivery, webhook, repo{id, full_name, default_branch, visibility}, data}`.

| Event | Asserted | Tests |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `landed` | exact keys, `change`, `count: 1`; durable delivery (bursts, restarts, no history for new hooks) | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks`, `webhooks.rs::webhooks_deliver_signed_events`, `webhooks.rs::landed_deliveries_survive_a_burst_larger_than_the_broadcast_channel`, `webhooks.rs::landings_while_the_daemon_was_down_are_delivered_after_a_restart`, `webhooks.rs::a_new_webhook_gets_no_history` |
| `change` | every `Change` field | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks`, `webhooks.rs::webhooks_deliver_signed_events` |
| `work` | exact `WorkItem` keys | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks`, `webhooks.rs::webhooks_deliver_signed_events` |
| `check` `{change, check}` | exact keys of the event and of `Check` | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks`, `webhooks.rs::commit_statuses_become_checks` |
| `check` `{changeset, status}` | exact keys, equal to the POST response | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks` |
| `preview` | `Preview` keys; deleting sends `preview: null` | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks` |
| `budget` | `{budget: {project, project_name, percent, spent_usd_month, max_usd_month}, dashboard_url}` (roll previews) | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks` |
| `queue` | exact `{state, paused, depth, batch}`; every state including `landing` | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks`, `queue.rs::pausing_lets_the_batch_under_test_finish` |
| `review` | exact `Review` keys (no `threads`); repeated once the `ai` record is attached | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks`, `webhooks.rs::webhooks_deliver_signed_events` |
| `thread` | exact `Thread` keys, linked to its review | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks` |
| `push` | created, refused (`accepted: false`, `reason`) and deleted refs | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks`, `webhooks.rs::pushes_are_webhook_events` |
| `ping` (webhooks only) | signed, on creation and on request | `webhooks.rs::webhooks_deliver_signed_events` |
| per-repo isolation, private feeds | | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks`, `routes.rs::every_repo_route_follows_the_auth_matrix` |
| stream limits: per token, per anonymous address, forwarded address when trusted, open daemons by address, server total, slot freed on close, refused requests take none, MCP event-stream answers share the slots | 429 with `Retry-After` and a JSON error | `streams.rs` (all) |

| `comment` | new thread (line, range, side new/old), reply, as an `X-Roll-Agent`; missing change and thread, malformed/unknown change, unknown thread, missing body/path, bad line/side; another owner may comment on a public repo; auth | `mcp.rs::tools_map_onto_their_routes`, `mcp.rs::tool_argument_errors`, `mcp.rs::every_tool_is_authorized_like_its_route` |
| `review` | approve with inline comments, request_changes, comment; reviewer from `X-Roll-Agent` / `X-Roll-User`; missing/bad verdict, bad comments, malformed/unknown change; another owner: comment allowed, approve "not allowed"; auth | `mcp.rs::tools_map_onto_their_routes`, `mcp.rs::tool_argument_errors`, `mcp.rs::every_tool_is_authorized_like_its_route` |
| `queue_status` | post_merge; queue repo (depth, urgent first) → same JSON as `GET /queue`; missing repo; repo errors; auth | `mcp.rs::tools_map_onto_their_routes`, `mcp.rs::land_outcomes_queued_branches_and_errors`, `mcp.rs::tool_argument_errors`, `mcp.rs::protocol_edges`, `mcp.rs::every_tool_is_authorized_like_its_route` |
| `request_ai_review` | queued job (mode, full) visible in `GET /ai-jobs`; bad mode, malformed/unknown change; auth | `mcp.rs::tools_map_onto_their_routes`, `mcp.rs::tool_argument_errors`, `mcp.rs::every_tool_is_authorized_like_its_route` |
| `list_agent_machines` | the owner's machines with `online`; another owner "not found" | `mcp.rs::agent_tools_drive_a_machine_like_their_routes` |
| `list_agent_threads` | threads of a machine (by name) with their machine and project name, and the pending requests | `mcp.rs::agent_tools_drive_a_machine_like_their_routes` |
| `create_agent_thread` | `thread.create` (provider, prompt, worktree) sent with the given id and the agent as `by`; the daemon's reply | `mcp.rs::agent_tools_drive_a_machine_like_their_routes` |
| `send_agent_message` | `message.send` (steer) with a fresh id; an unknown machine name "not found" | `mcp.rs::agent_tools_drive_a_machine_like_their_routes` |
| `respond_agent_request` | a decision with a message, answers; neither is a failure | `mcp.rs::agent_tools_drive_a_machine_like_their_routes` |
| `interrupt_agent_thread` | `run.interrupt`; the daemon's `conflict` is a result, not a failure; another owner "not allowed", anonymous "needs a token", nothing sent | `mcp.rs::agent_tools_drive_a_machine_like_their_routes` |
| `publish_agent_thread` | `thread.publish` with a push grant for the project's repo; the daemon's `published` reply | `mcp.rs::agent_tools_work_in_roll_code_like_their_routes` |
| `create_agent_project` | the repo is created (visibility) and `project.add` clones it with a fetch grant | `mcp.rs::agent_tools_work_in_roll_code_like_their_routes` |
| `claim_agent_work` | the next work item claimed as the agent and set on the thread; `work: null` when none is left; an unknown thread "not found" | `mcp.rs::agent_tools_work_in_roll_code_like_their_routes` |

| `list_agent_automations` | the owner's automations | `mcp.rs::automation_tools_map_onto_their_routes` |
| `save_agent_automation` | creates, or replaces by id over a revision | `mcp.rs::automation_tools_map_onto_their_routes` |
| `run_agent_automation` | by id or name, with `input` and `key`; an agent only when `allow_agents` | `mcp.rs::automation_tools_map_onto_their_routes` |
| `list_agent_automation_runs` | newest first, by automation | `mcp.rs::automation_tools_map_onto_their_routes` |
| `get_agent_automation_run` | the run and its event | `mcp.rs::automation_tools_map_onto_their_routes` |
| `cancel_agent_automation_run` | the owner's; an agent is refused | `mcp.rs::automation_tools_map_onto_their_routes` |

### Protocol

| Behaviour | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| sessions (400 without, 404 unknown, ended, bound to the token), version negotiation, `initialized`, ping, tools/list, unknown method and tool, non-object arguments, parse errors, wrong `jsonrpc`, batches with notifications, `MCP-Protocol-Version`, Origin, `GET` 405, `DELETE` | `mcp.rs::transport_sessions_and_protocol` |
| tools enforce the routes' authorization: anonymous public reads, private 404, writes need a token, another owner can't write, admin can | `mcp.rs::every_tool_is_authorized_like_its_route` |
| every tool's schema is closed and its required fields exist; path and query encoding | `code-control/src/mcp.rs` unit tests |
| `Accept: text/event-stream`: one `message` event per response (single and batch), notifications 202, sessions still required, initialize answers JSON | `mcp.rs::responses_as_server_sent_events` |
| a revoked token ends its session (401) and can't start one | `tokens.rs::tokens_are_listed_and_revoked_everywhere_at_once` |
| all four versions and the default, initialize inside a batch (400), empty batch, client responses and notification-only batches (202), one bad entry in a batch, `resources/list` and `prompts/list`, `tools/call` without arguments or a name, Origin edge cases, `GET` `Allow`, `DELETE` without a session | `mcp.rs::protocol_edges` |
| a never-valid token: 401 on initialize, and it ends the session it's used on; anonymous callers can't use or end another token's session | `mcp.rs::invalid_tokens_and_foreign_sessions` |
| `tools/list` is exactly the 22 tools of mcp.md, each with `readOnlyHint` | `mcp.rs::transport_sessions_and_protocol` |
| event-stream answers hold one of the caller's stream slots (429 past the limit) | `streams.rs::mcp_event_stream_answers_share_the_callers_slots` |

## CLI (`roll code`)

The CLI is covered by `roll-cli/tests/code.rs`, which runs the real binary against a live API, and
the unit tests in `roll-cli/src/code.rs`. Exit codes: 0 success, 1 any error (usage errors
included), 2 a conflict to rebase, 3 `work claim` with nothing to claim.

| Command | Behaviours / exit codes covered | Tests |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `roll code owners` | list text and `--json` | `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::owners_repos_and_reading`, `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::repo_land_and_usage_edges` |
| `roll code owners create` | `--org`, `--user`, `--display-name`; taken and invalid exit 1 | `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::owners_repos_and_reading`, `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::repo_land_and_usage_edges` |
| `roll code name` | available exit 0; taken/reserved/invalid exit 1 with the reason | `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::owners_repos_and_reading` |
| `roll code repos` | `--owner`, all, `ls-repos` alias, empty, closed pipe | `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::owners_repos_and_reading`, `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::repo_land_and_usage_edges`, `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::a_closed_pipe_ends_quietly` |
| `roll code create` | `--private`, `-d`, `--default-branch`; taken exit 1, bad name; `--github` without `--installation` refused; `--github` malformed or no App: exit 1 | `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::owners_repos_and_reading`, `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::repo_land_and_usage_edges`, `roll-cli` `code::tests::args` |
| `roll code repo` | text (empty head, clone, landing policy, stats); not found and malformed exit 1 | `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::owners_repos_and_reading`, `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::repo_land_and_usage_edges` |

## CLI (`roll agent`, `roll-cli/tests/agent.rs`)

The real binary against code-control's relay on a free port; the daemon's Codex points at a
binary that doesn't exist, so no provider runs.

| Command | Covered | Tests |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `roll agent login` | prints the code and the dashboard URL, sends hostname and OS (the pending lookup shows them), polls until approved, writes `agent.json` 0600 (api, machine, owner, name, token); `--json`; denied: exit 1, nothing written | `roll-cli/tests/agent.rs::login_up_status_and_logout`, `roll-cli/tests/agent.rs::a_denied_login_fails` |
| `roll agent login --machine-token` | a dashboard-minted `rlm_…` token (or `ROLL_AGENT_TOKEN`): asks roll which machine it is, writes the same file (`--json` and text); not an `rlm_` token, a revoked one, or with `--name`: fails, nothing written | `roll-cli/tests/agent.rs::a_machine_token_logs_in_without_a_code` |
| `roll agent up` | opens the daemon, connects (online, `hello` info with providers), answers commands from the API, Ctrl-C stops it cleanly (exit 0, offline); a revoked machine stops it with exit 1 and "roll agent login"; without a login file it takes `ROLL_AGENT_TOKEN` (a sandbox) and re-execs without it, so neither its terminals nor its own `/proc/<pid>/environ` hold it (also when a login file wins); a token that isn't a machine token is refused | `roll-cli/tests/agent.rs::login_up_status_and_logout`, `roll-cli/tests/agent.rs::a_revoked_machine_stops_up_with_a_clear_error`, `roll-cli/tests/agent.rs::up_takes_a_sandbox_token_from_the_environment` |
| `roll agent up` on SIGTERM (a sandbox going to sleep) | stops cleanly (exit 0) with a run that waits on an approval still waiting on roll; after the next start the approval is answerable, the conversation resumes, the repeated ask is answered at once and the run completes | `roll-cli/tests/agent.rs::sigterm_parks_a_waiting_run_and_the_answer_resumes_it_after_a_restart` |
| `roll agent status` | not logged in (exit 1), accepted (`--json`), revoked (exit 1) | `roll-cli/tests/agent.rs::login_up_status_and_logout`, `roll-cli/tests/agent.rs::a_revoked_machine_stops_up_with_a_clear_error` |
| `roll agent logout` | revokes the machine with its own token (`DELETE /v1/code/agents/connect`) and deletes the file | `roll-cli/tests/agent.rs::login_up_status_and_logout` |

## Merge queue (`code-control/tests/queue.rs`, fake runner)

### Transitions

Every entry state (`waiting`, `testing`, `bisecting`, `landing`, `landed`, `ejected`), queue state and
change state transition `src/queue.rs` makes.

| Transition | Trigger | Tests |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `(none) → waiting` | enqueue | `queue.rs::batches_land_together_in_fifo_order` |
| `waiting → waiting` | re-enqueue (no-op), lane change | `queue.rs::lanes_can_change_without_losing_place` |
| `waiting → testing` | batch built | `queue.rs::the_batch_is_rebuilt_when_main_moves_under_it`, `queue.rs::lanes_can_change_without_losing_place` |
| `testing → landing → landed` | green batch | `queue.rs::batches_land_together_in_fifo_order`, `queue.rs::pausing_lets_the_batch_under_test_finish` |
| `testing → landed` | head already on main | `queue.rs::a_change_already_on_main_counts_as_landed` |
| `testing → ejected` | conflict | `queue.rs::conflicts_are_ejected_with_their_paths` |
| `testing → bisecting` | red batch | `queue.rs::dequeueing_during_a_bisect_rebuilds_the_batch`, `queue.rs::a_red_batch_is_bisected_to_its_culprit` |
| `testing → bisecting → ejected` | timeout | `queue.rs::a_batch_that_runs_out_of_time_is_ejected` |
| `testing → waiting` | dequeue; revision (stays queued) | `queue.rs::dequeueing_during_a_bisect_rebuilds_the_batch`, `queue.rs::revising_or_closing_a_queued_change` |
| `testing → removed` | close, draft, PR closed on GitHub | `queue.rs::revising_or_closing_a_queued_change`, `queue.rs::blockers_keep_changes_out_of_the_queue`, `github.rs::pull_request_issue_and_installation_actions` |
| `testing → waiting` | left the queue or head moved by landing time | `queue.rs::a_batch_never_lands_a_change_that_left_the_queue` |
| `testing → waiting` | a blocker appeared during the test | `queue.rs::a_check_going_red_during_the_test_keeps_the_change_off_main` |
| `testing → landing → waiting` | main moved (CAS failed) | `queue.rs::the_batch_is_rebuilt_when_main_moves_under_it` |
| `testing → waiting` | restart; a failed step | `queue.rs::a_restart_during_a_bisect_retests_the_batch`, `queue.rs::a_failed_step_puts_the_batch_back_and_retries` |
| `bisecting → landing → landed` | the prefix before the culprit | `queue.rs::a_red_batch_is_bisected_to_its_culprit` |
| `bisecting → ejected` | failing check (culprit), two culprits | `queue.rs::a_red_batch_is_bisected_to_its_culprit`, `queue.rs::throughput_with_culprits` |
| `bisecting → waiting` | after the culprit; dequeue mid-bisect; main moved; restart | `queue.rs::a_red_batch_is_bisected_to_its_culprit`, `queue.rs::dequeueing_during_a_bisect_rebuilds_the_batch`, `queue.rs::main_moving_while_a_batch_is_bisected_retests_everything`, `queue.rs::a_restart_during_a_bisect_retests_the_batch` |
| `landing → landed` / `landing → waiting` | restart, tip on main or not | `queue.rs::a_restart_finishes_a_batch_that_had_landed` |
| `waiting → ejected` | gate: untrusted revision, check red, draft, closed, change gone | `queue.rs::changes_that_stop_being_mergeable_while_waiting_are_ejected`, `queue.rs::blockers_that_appear_while_waiting_eject_at_the_gate` |
| `waiting → removed` | draft; PR closed, merged or back to draft on GitHub | `queue.rs::blockers_keep_changes_out_of_the_queue`, `github.rs::pull_request_issue_and_installation_actions` |
| `ejected → waiting` | re-enqueue | `queue.rs::conflicts_are_ejected_with_their_paths` |
| queue state `idle` | | `queue.rs::batches_land_together_in_fifo_order` |
| queue state `paused` | pause, also over a finishing batch | `queue.rs::pausing_lets_the_batch_under_test_finish` |
| queue state `testing` | | `queue.rs::the_batch_is_rebuilt_when_main_moves_under_it` |
| queue state `bisecting` | | `queue.rs::dequeueing_during_a_bisect_rebuilds_the_batch` |
| queue state `landing` | event while the CAS runs | `queue.rs::pausing_lets_the_batch_under_test_finish` |
| batch size: halve on red, double on green, cap, floor 1, a lowered max | | `queue.rs::the_batch_size_halves_on_red_and_doubles_on_green` |
| change `open → queued` | enqueue | `queue.rs::batches_land_together_in_fifo_order` |
| change `queued → landed` | batch landed, already on main, merged on GitHub | `queue.rs::batches_land_together_in_fifo_order`, `queue.rs::a_change_already_on_main_counts_as_landed`, `github.rs::pull_request_issue_and_installation_actions` |
| change `queued → conflict` | conflict ejection | `queue.rs::conflicts_are_ejected_with_their_paths` |
| change `queued → open` | ejected (check, gate, timeout), dequeue, draft | `queue.rs::a_red_batch_is_bisected_to_its_culprit`, `queue.rs::dequeueing_during_a_bisect_rebuilds_the_batch`, `queue.rs::blockers_that_appear_while_waiting_eject_at_the_gate`, `queue.rs::blockers_keep_changes_out_of_the_queue` |
| change `queued → queued` | revision | `queue.rs::revising_or_closing_a_queued_change` |
| change `queued → closed` | close in roll or on GitHub; a closed change stays closed at the gate | `queue.rs::revising_or_closing_a_queued_change`, `github.rs::pull_request_issue_and_installation_actions`, `queue.rs::blockers_that_appear_while_waiting_eject_at_the_gate` |
| change `conflict → queued` / `conflict → open` | re-enqueue / revise | `queue.rs::conflicts_are_ejected_with_their_paths` |
| change `landed` or `closed` → queued refused | 409 | `queue.rs::blockers_keep_changes_out_of_the_queue` |

### Behaviours

| Behaviour | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| batching: one test for the batch, FIFO landing, positions, `queue_id`, `recent`, `landed_1h`, `last_green`, merges counted per changeset, scratch refs hidden, `queue_depth` | `batches_land_together_in_fifo_order` |
| lanes: urgent before normal, re-enqueue is a no-op, bad lane 400 | `lanes_can_change_without_losing_place` |
| red batch: bisected to one culprit (prefix lands, rest re-queued, a one-line `error` and the failing check's log tail in `output`), batch size adapts | `a_red_batch_is_bisected_to_its_culprit` |
| moving lanes keeps the entry and `enqueued_at`, positions follow the lane order, audited; no lane 400, bad lane 400, not queued 409 | `lanes_can_change_without_losing_place` |
| `batch.testing_prefix` (the whole batch, then the half being bisected), `Repo.stats.queue_batch`, `output` on the change and in `recent` | `dequeueing_during_a_bisect_rebuilds_the_batch`, `a_red_batch_is_bisected_to_its_culprit` |
| two culprits | `throughput_with_culprits` |
| a check already red on the head doesn't count against the batch; its fix gets through | `a_check_that_fails_on_main_too_doesnt_eject_anyone` |
| conflicts ejected with paths (state `conflict`); revised, it lands | `conflicts_are_ejected_with_their_paths` |
| the default branch moves under a batch (admin bypass): rebuilt, audit entry | `the_batch_is_rebuilt_when_main_moves_under_it` |
| bypass: admin only (403 for an owner), owner writes queued with `enqueued_by`, audit visibility, anonymous can't pause, anyone can read a public queue | `bypass_needs_the_admin_token` |
| pause and resume (the worker waits), audited | `pausing_lets_the_batch_under_test_finish` |
| dequeue mid-test (batch rebuilt), dequeue twice 409 | `dequeueing_during_a_bisect_rebuilds_the_batch` |
| revise while queued (stays queued, retested), close while queued (leaves) | `revising_or_closing_a_queued_change` |
| the batch shows in landing order (urgent first, in flight and waiting), with positions and its batch id | `lanes_can_change_without_losing_place` |
| landing re-checks that every change is still queued at the tested head | `a_batch_never_lands_a_change_that_left_the_queue` |
| the default branch moves while a batch is bisected: everything is tested again, nothing gets stuck | `main_moving_while_a_batch_is_bisected_retests_everything` |
| deleting a repo stops its queue worker and drops its entries | `deleting_a_repo_stops_its_queue_worker` |
| restart: the batch under test is tested again | `a_restart_during_a_bisect_retests_the_batch` |
| restart: a `landing` entry whose tip is on main counts as landed (once), one whose tip isn't goes back in line | `a_restart_finishes_a_batch_that_had_landed` |
| git push to main and to `refs/land/main` on a queue repo: `ng queued as change #N (position P)`, `remote:` hints, no duplicate change for the same head; other branches untouched | `git_pushes_to_main_are_queued` |
| blockers: draft, checks (`required_checks` and `require_change_checks`), untrusted (approve-run, reset on revision), closed 409 | `blockers_keep_changes_out_of_the_queue` |
| a change that stops being mergeable while it waits is ejected | `changes_that_stop_being_mergeable_while_waiting_are_ejected` |
| `GET …/queue` shape, settings from roll.toml (and a broken one), `queue` SSE event shape, 404, `land_policy` validation | `a_check_table_the_runner_rejects_shows_in_the_settings`, `pausing_lets_the_batch_under_test_finish`, `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks` |
| a `[checks.*]` table the runner rejects (e.g. `timeout = "60s"`) shows as the queue settings' error (`Runner::validate`) | `a_check_table_the_runner_rejects_shows_in_the_settings`, `config.rs::errors_name_the_key` |
| post_merge repos: drafts block landing; the queue still works when asked | `blockers_keep_changes_out_of_the_queue` |
| reverts: inverse diff restores modified, deleted and added files, a directory and the mode; later work on the same file kept; links and finishes the work item; the first changeset can't be reverted | `a_revert_restores_the_parent_and_keeps_later_work` |
| a suggested revert (no `auto_revert`) leaves its broken-main item open, never passing through `review`; a revert that will land starts the work | `a_revert_restores_the_parent_and_keeps_later_work`, `webhooks.rs::failing_statuses_on_the_default_branch_break_main` |
| a revert on a queue repo is queued urgent, ahead of waiting changes | `a_revert_on_a_queue_repo_jumps_the_line` |
| `find_culprit`: none without a known green head; bisects 16 changesets in ≤ 4 test runs | `find_culprit_bisects_from_the_last_green_head` |
| 2,000 changes with 2% culprits: all others land exactly once, in queue order, on a linear main (`CODE_QUEUE_N`; 60 in `pnpm test:code`, 2,000 with `--stress`) | `throughput_with_culprits` |
| `Store::advance`: one merge and one event per changeset, refuses a moved ref or a tip that isn't on top, creates a missing ref, keeps the path index exact; scratch landings never listed, counted or published | `code-store/tests/advance.rs` (all) |
| `[queue]` and `[main]` parsing and errors (both parsers) | `code-control/src/settings.rs` tests, `code-preview/src/config.rs::queue_and_main_tables`, `config.rs::errors_name_the_key` |

| blocker `unresolved_threads` | appears/clears (roll and GitHub resolve, GitHub unresolve brings it back) | `review.rs::every_rule_on_and_off_from_settings_and_the_file`, `github.rs::review_events_every_action_against_the_rules` |
| blocker `review_pending` | appears/clears (review of head, new head, by hand, disabled or deleted app) | `review.rs::review_requests_and_pending_reviews`, `review.rs::review_apps_events_scopes_requests_and_failed_deliveries` |
| blocker `review_required` | appears (`/land`, git push, required app)/clears (approved change lands, app removed) | `review.rs::direct_landings_and_pushes_need_review`, `review.rs::review_apps_events_scopes_requests_and_failed_deliveries` |

### GitHub review events

| Event | Covered | Tests |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `pull_request_review.submitted` | OWNER (any case), MEMBER and COLLABORATOR decide; NONE, CONTRIBUTOR or missing → comment; a bot's verdict kept, counts only via `count_ai_approval`; the PR author's own doesn't count; pending ignored; roll's own echo ignored; redelivery once; non-head or unknown commit → stale; unknown PR ignored; settles the review request; links earlier inline comments | `github.rs::reviews_both_ways`, `github.rs::review_events_every_action_against_the_rules`, `code-github/tests/events.rs::review_events` |
| `pull_request_review.edited` | known: body updated in place; unknown: recorded | `github.rs::reviews_both_ways`, `github.rs::review_events_every_action_against_the_rules`, `code-github/tests/events.rs::review_events` |
| `pull_request_review.dismissed` | stops counting or blocking, `dismissed_by @sender`, redelivery no-op, unknown ignored | `github.rs::reviews_both_ways`, `github.rs::review_events_every_action_against_the_rules`, `code-github/tests/events.rs::review_events` |
| `pull_request_review_comment.created` | thread (RIGHT→new, LEFT→old, no line→outdated), suggestion, pending review link, replies once, `@roll` commands | `github.rs::reviews_both_ways`, `github.rs::review_events_every_action_against_the_rules`, `github.rs::roll_commands_from_github_comments`, `code-github/tests/events.rs::review_events` |
| `pull_request_review_comment.edited` | in place; unknown no-op | `github.rs::reviews_both_ways`, `github.rs::review_events_every_action_against_the_rules`, `code-github/tests/events.rs::review_events` |
| `pull_request_review_comment.deleted` | removed; unknown no-op | `github.rs::reviews_both_ways`, `github.rs::review_events_every_action_against_the_rules`, `code-github/tests/events.rs::review_events` |
| `pull_request_review_thread.resolved` | resolved by @sender, node id kept, not echoed; roll's own and unknown ignored | `github.rs::reviews_both_ways`, `github.rs::review_events_every_action_against_the_rules`, `code-github/tests/events.rs::review_events` |
| `pull_request_review_thread.unresolved` | unresolved in roll, blocker returns, not echoed; unknown ignored | `github.rs::review_events_every_action_against_the_rules`, `code-github/tests/events.rs::review_events` |

### Behaviours

| Behaviour | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| required approvals; self-approval; org token, `agent:` token and `X-Roll-Agent` identities; request changes blocks until superseded or dismissed (409 twice, audited); a comment doesn't lift it; stale approvals with and without `dismiss_stale`; `allow_self_approval`; bad input 400s; a landed change takes no reviews (409) | `every_rule_on_and_off_from_settings_and_the_file`, `codeowners_locations_last_match_and_every_owner_kind`, `every_review_route_answers_every_status`, `rules::request_changes_blocks_until_superseded` |
| dismissing a reviewer's latest change request doesn't revive their older one (a dismissed review supersedes, counts for nothing) | `rules::a_dismissed_review_doesnt_bring_back_an_older_one` |
| a change's base stays where it left main when revisions stack on its head (applied suggestions, a second pushed commit) | `threads_suggestions_and_carry_forward`, `git.rs` (branch push revises) |
| the review provider reads as nulls (hosted) for its owner, 404 for anyone else | `reviewer.rs` (caps and BYOK), `code-api.test.ts` |
| roll's reviewer approvals and `count_ai_approval`; `land_change` blocked (422), owner bypass 403, admin bypass lands and is audited | `ai_approvals_and_land_change_gate` |
| CODEOWNERS groups and messages, `agent:` owners, the file can't lower repo settings, unknown keys reported, auto-approve by paths and comment-only, requested changes still block auto-approved changes, merge-base paths (a trailing docs commit doesn't hide code), a broken review file falls back | `codeowners_auto_approve_and_the_review_file` |
| threads: validation (path, lines, body, side, traversal), replies, resolve/unresolve, `require_resolved_threads`, suggestions applied (single and multi-line, writers only, old side 422, none 422), carry forward (moved, kept, outdated → 409) | `threads_suggestions_and_carry_forward` |
| `/land` and `git push` (main and `refs/land/main`) open a change when reviews are required; owner bypass 403; auto-approved paths land directly; other branches aren't gated | `direct_landings_and_pushes_need_review` |
| queue: enqueue blocked until approved, re-blocked after a revision, `/land` on a queue repo checks reviews | `the_queue_waits_for_approval` |
| review requests: required → `review_pending`, roll's review clears it, per head, by hand; bad input; writers only | `review_requests_and_pending_reviews` |
| review apps: validation, owner-only management, token shown once, scoped reads (private installed / not installed), no writes, signed `change.opened`, `review.requested` for required apps, diff URL, app reviews clear the request, no echo of its own comments, retries with backoff, delivery log, redeliver, update/disable/rotate/delete | `review_apps_lifecycle_and_deliveries` |
| app tokens are scoped on an open daemon; bogus app tokens 401 | `app_tokens_are_scoped_even_on_an_open_daemon` |
| webhooks to private addresses (IP and `localhost`) are refused | `private_webhook_addresses_are_refused` |
| `review`, `thread` and `change` events; `compare?format=` json, diff, bad 400 | `webhooks.rs::every_event_is_the_same_on_the_stream_and_in_webhooks`, `review_apps_lifecycle_and_deliveries`, `routes.rs::statuses_of_every_route` |
| every review route × anonymous / another owner / the repo's owner, public and private; dismissing own vs others'; resolving own threads | `every_review_route_answers_every_status` |
| GitHub: CodeRabbit review → app review blocking the change, redelivery, edit, pending ignored, echo ignored; Greptile inline comment with suggestion → thread linked to its review when it arrives, replies (once), edit, delete, resolve, dismiss; roll review → one GitHub review with inline comments, keyed so GitHub replies land in roll's thread; standalone threads and replies posted once; refused approvals fall back to a comment | `reviews_both_ways` |
| gitignore/CODEOWNERS patterns, CODEOWNERS parsing and owners, the rules evaluation, `.roll/review.yaml` parsing and tightening, comment-only detection per language (strings with markers, lifetimes, raw strings, docstrings, indentation), line diffs, carry-forward, suggestions, line replacement, private address ranges, URL checks, signatures | `review::{pattern,codeowners,rules,comments,lines,apps}` unit tests |
| GitHub review webhook parsing (every action, ignored ones, malformed) | `code-github/tests/events.rs::review_events` |
| CLI: `review` (status, comment with inline comments as an agent, approve, request, request-changes, dismiss, bad action), `thread` (list, open, reply, resolve, unresolve, apply), `review-settings`, `review-apps` (create, list, show, update, rotate, deliveries, redeliver, delete) | `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::reviews_threads_and_review_apps`, `code::review` unit tests |
| `GET /v1/code` `auth.caller.reviewer` (agent token, `X-Roll-Agent`, `X-Roll-User`, anonymous); an unplaceable inline comment → 400 `{comment: index}` and nothing written; review requests carry the `Reviewer` object; `review-settings` with no file and no CODEOWNERS (`file: null`, `codeowners.path: null`); review apps without `repos` are `all_repos`; renamed files: old side takes the old path, new side the new path, the other combinations 400 | `contract_details_caller_comment_index_renames_and_apps` |
| review apps decide (approve, request changes) only on repos they're installed on; a disabled or deleted required app owes nothing | `review.rs::review_apps_events_scopes_requests_and_failed_deliveries` |

## roll's reviewer (`code-control/tests/reviewer.rs`, `code-review/tests/engine.rs`, `code-index/tests/index.rs`, unit tests)

No test calls a real model API: Anthropic and OpenAI-compatible endpoints are mocks that record
every request.

### Modes, speeds and caps

| Feature | Covered | Tests |
| --- | --- | --- |
| mode `standard` | no repository context, effort medium, no verification | `reviewer.rs::reviews_a_change_automatically_with_the_right_request`, `engine.rs::effort_follows_the_table_for_every_mode_and_speed` |
| mode `deep` | repository context, confidence shown, effort high, goes on without context while the index is first built | `reviewer.rs::auto_mode_deep_context_and_classifier`, `reviewer.rs::a_deep_review_goes_on_without_context_while_the_index_is_first_built` |
| mode `max` | verification drops unconfirmed findings | `reviewer.rs::fast_streams_threads_and_max_verifies`, `reviewer.rs::modes_speeds_effort_classifier_fallback_and_profiles`, `engine.rs::max_mode_verifies_and_uses_context` |
| mode `auto` | `deep_paths` → deep, `max_paths` → max, small change → standard | `reviewer.rs::auto_mode_deep_context_and_classifier`, `reviewer.rs::modes_speeds_effort_classifier_fallback_and_profiles`, `code-review` `mode::tests::routing` |
| chosen_by `auto-heuristic` | including the fallback when the classifier call fails | `reviewer.rs::auto_mode_deep_context_and_classifier`, `reviewer.rs::modes_speeds_effort_classifier_fallback_and_profiles` |
| chosen_by `auto-model` | classifier on the secondary model at low effort, metered | `reviewer.rs::auto_mode_deep_context_and_classifier`, `engine.rs::classifier_and_replies` |
| chosen_by `settings` | a fixed mode | `reviewer.rs::reviews_a_change_automatically_with_the_right_request`, `reviewer.rs::roll_commands_from_writers_act_and_from_readers_are_ignored` |
| chosen_by `command` | `@roll review deep`, `@roll full review max`, `mode` in POST ai-review | `reviewer.rs::roll_commands_in_threads`, `reviewer.rs::roll_commands_from_writers_act_and_from_readers_are_ignored` |
| speed `fast` | secondary model, low effort, groups in parallel, threads streamed, no verification | `reviewer.rs::fast_streams_threads_and_max_verifies`, `engine.rs::fast_runs_groups_in_parallel_and_streams`, `engine.rs::effort_follows_the_table_for_every_mode_and_speed` |
| speed `balanced` | standard → medium, deep and max → high | `engine.rs::effort_follows_the_table_for_every_mode_and_speed`, `reviewer.rs::auto_mode_deep_context_and_classifier` |
| speed `thorough` | xhigh, verifies even standard reviews | `engine.rs::effort_follows_the_table_for_every_mode_and_speed`, `reviewer.rs::modes_speeds_effort_classifier_fallback_and_profiles` |
| effort table (9 mode × speed cells) and the `effort` override | override checked in every cell | `engine.rs::effort_follows_the_table_for_every_mode_and_speed`, `reviewer.rs::modes_speeds_effort_classifier_fallback_and_profiles` |
| profile `chill` / `assertive` | chill drops nits (approve); assertive posts them (comment) | `reviewer.rs::modes_speeds_effort_classifier_fallback_and_profiles`, `engine.rs::dedupes_against_open_threads_and_profiles` |
| incremental vs `full` | | `reviewer.rs::incremental_re_reviews_dedupe_and_skips`, `reviewer.rs::change_reviewer_routes_answer_every_status_and_jobs_queue`, `reviewer.rs::roll_commands_from_writers_act_and_from_readers_are_ignored` |
| on_cap `pause` | no model call, comment, job skipped, change stops waiting, nothing metered | `reviewer.rs::on_cap_pause_byok_fallback_and_standard_only`, `reviewer.rs::pricing_caps_allowances_and_byok` |
| on_cap `byok_fallback` | without a key: paused, chats refused; with a key: the owner's key at BYOK prices | `reviewer.rs::on_cap_pause_byok_fallback_and_standard_only` |
| on_cap `standard_only` | deep/max → standard; standard goes on past the cap and is charged; chats refused | `reviewer.rs::on_cap_pause_byok_fallback_and_standard_only`, `reviewer.rs::pricing_caps_allowances_and_byok` |

### Commands

| Command | Covered | Tests |
| --- | --- | --- |
| command `@roll review` | "On it"; skipped when nothing changed; resumes a paused change | `reviewer.rs::roll_commands_from_writers_act_and_from_readers_are_ignored` |
| command `@roll review [mode]` | forced mode | `reviewer.rs::roll_commands_in_threads` |
| command `@roll full review [mode]` | everything reviewed | `reviewer.rs::roll_commands_from_writers_act_and_from_readers_are_ignored` |

| command `@roll explain` | metered as a chat | `reviewer.rs::roll_commands_in_threads` |
| command `@roll fix` | a revision with the suggestion | `reviewer.rs::roll_commands_in_threads` |

| command: a question (`@roll <anything>`) | | `reviewer.rs::roll_commands_in_threads`, `reviewer.rs::config_and_key_errors_give_up_without_retrying` |
| command from a non-writer | all 13 forms ignored: no reply, job, model call, learning, pause or resolve | `reviewer.rs::roll_commands_from_writers_act_and_from_readers_are_ignored`, `reviewer.rs::roll_commands_in_threads` |

### Errors

| Error | Covered | Tests |
| --- | --- | --- |
| error `bad key (401)` | not retried, reported, change no longer waits; a chat says why in its thread | `reviewer.rs::provider_errors_403_persistent_529_and_400`, `engine.rs::every_group_failing_is_an_error_and_stop_reasons_are_read` |
| error `bad key (403)` | | `reviewer.rs::provider_errors_403_persistent_529_and_400`, `engine.rs::timeouts_forbidden_server_errors_and_garbage` |
| error `429` | `retry-after` honoured | `engine.rs::retries_429_with_retry_after_but_not_400` |
| error `529` | retried by the provider, then by the job (3 attempts), then gives up | `engine.rs::retries_429_with_retry_after_but_not_400`, `reviewer.rs::provider_errors_403_persistent_529_and_400` |
| error `5xx` | | `reviewer.rs::provider_errors_403_persistent_529_and_400`, `engine.rs::timeouts_forbidden_server_errors_and_garbage`, `engine.rs::retries_429_with_retry_after_but_not_400` |
| error `400` | not retried; one failing group is listed and the rest reviewed | `engine.rs::retries_429_with_retry_after_but_not_400`, `reviewer.rs::provider_errors_403_persistent_529_and_400`, `engine.rs::a_failing_group_is_listed_and_the_rest_is_reviewed` |
| error `timeout` | retried, then exhausted | `engine.rs::timeouts_forbidden_server_errors_and_garbage` |
| error `refusal` | files listed, no approval | `reviewer.rs::refusals_and_cut_off_reviews_list_the_files_and_dont_approve`, `engine.rs::max_tokens_splits_and_refusals_skip`, `engine.rs::a_failing_group_is_listed_and_the_rest_is_reviewed` |
| error `max_tokens` | split and retried; what still doesn't fit is listed | `reviewer.rs::refusals_and_cut_off_reviews_list_the_files_and_dont_approve`, `engine.rs::max_tokens_splits_and_refusals_skip` |
| error: undecodable, oversized or redirected answer | | `engine.rs::timeouts_forbidden_server_errors_and_garbage`, `engine.rs::oversized_answers_and_redirects_are_refused` |
| error `config error` | | `reviewer.rs::config_and_key_errors_give_up_without_retrying`, `provider::openai` unit tests |
| error `no provider` | review and chat | `reviewer.rs::provider_errors_403_persistent_529_and_400`, `reviewer.rs::config_and_key_errors_give_up_without_retrying` |
| error `invalid settings` | 400 names the field; `.roll/review.yaml` errors reported | `reviewer.rs::repo_reviewer_routes_answer_every_status`, `reviewer.rs::triggers_settings_and_auth`, `code-review` `settings::tests::overlay_and_errors` |
| error `ROLL_SECRET_KEY` missing or weak | when setting a key | `reviewer.rs::byok_needs_a_server_secret` |
| error `ROLL_SECRET_KEY` missing or changed at review time | no fallback to the hosted key | `reviewer.rs::config_and_key_errors_give_up_without_retrying` |

| Job | Covered | Tests |
| --- | --- | --- |

| job state `running` | one per change | `reviewer.rs::change_reviewer_routes_answer_every_status_and_jobs_queue`, `reviewer.rs::jobs_go_queued_running_reviewing_verifying_done` |
| job state `done` | | `reviewer.rs::jobs_go_queued_running_reviewing_verifying_done`, `reviewer.rs::byok_openai_compatible_end_to_end` |
| job state `failed` | | `reviewer.rs::provider_errors_403_persistent_529_and_400`, `reviewer.rs::config_and_key_errors_give_up_without_retrying` |
| job state `skipped` | | `reviewer.rs::incremental_re_reviews_dedupe_and_skips`, `reviewer.rs::change_reviewer_routes_answer_every_status_and_jobs_queue`, `reviewer.rs::on_cap_pause_byok_fallback_and_standard_only` |
| job stages `queued` → `indexing` → `chunking` → `reviewing` → `verifying` → `posting` → `done` / `failed` | in order; `posting` lasts microseconds, checked as an allowed value | `reviewer.rs::jobs_go_queued_running_reviewing_verifying_done`, `engine.rs::max_mode_verifies_and_uses_context` |
| restart recovery | | `reviewer.rs::restart_recovers_running_jobs` |
| spend caps under concurrency | reviews, chats and free public reviews reserve before the model call; the same races with the jobs on two nodes; 32 reservations at once across two nodes; a dead node's reservation lapses; metering releases in the same transaction | `reviewer.rs::concurrent_reviews_and_chats_never_overrun_the_cap`, `reviewer.rs::concurrent_public_reviews_on_two_nodes_never_share_the_last_free_one`, `code-control/src/ai/mod.rs::reservations_hold_the_cap_across_nodes`, `code-control/src/ai/mod.rs::reservations_of_a_dead_node_lapse` |

## GitHub

### Webhook events

Parsing (`code-github/tests`) and the effect in roll against a mock GitHub
(`code-control/tests/github.rs`). Review events are under Reviews.

### Outbound and the mirror

| outbound: a PR already closed on GitHub gets no write | `github.rs::outbound_notifications_once_each` |
| outbound: GitHub's own close or merge isn't echoed | `github.rs::pull_request_issue_and_installation_actions` |
| outbound close the issue `completed` / `not_planned`; an issue closed on GitHub isn't written to | `github.rs::connect_import_webhooks_and_mirror`, `github.rs::outbound_notifications_once_each` |
| outbound check runs: create, update, all 6 statuses; GitHub-sourced and non-PR checks aren't sent | `github.rs::connect_import_webhooks_and_mirror`, `github.rs::outbound_notifications_once_each` |
| outbound `@roll` answers (PR comment, thread reply) | `github.rs::roll_commands_from_github_comments` |
| outbound review posted as one GitHub review, threads resolved both ways | `github.rs::reviews_both_ways`, `github.rs::roll_reviewer_posts_to_the_pull_request` |
| mirror `ok` | `github.rs::connect_import_webhooks_and_mirror` |
| mirror `pending` (roll ahead) | `github.rs::push_webhooks_for_branches_tags_and_echoes` |
| mirror `error` → `ok` | `github.rs::connect_import_webhooks_and_mirror` |
| mirror `paused` (overlap, rewritten history, disconnected) and back | `github.rs::push_to_github_main_is_ingested_and_merged`, `github.rs::push_webhooks_for_branches_tags_and_echoes`, `github.rs::pull_request_issue_and_installation_actions` |
| mirror coalescing (200 landings ≤ 10 pushes), stops on delete; scheduler: max delay, backoff, lost responses, GitHub ahead, diverged | `github.rs::connect_import_webhooks_and_mirror`, `code-github/tests/mirror.rs` (all) |
| GitHub down: a landed PR's close and comment wait in the delivery queue (`gh_…`, retried with backoff, never given up while GitHub doesn't answer) and reach GitHub once after it's back | `github.rs::a_landed_prs_close_reaches_github_after_an_outage` |
| rate limits on the way out: inside the outbox a `retry-after` is an error with its reset at once (the delivery waits until then) | `code-github/tests/github.rs::rate_limits_and_retries` |
| mirror backoff capped at 30 s, however long GitHub was down | `code-github/tests/mirror.rs::a_long_outage_retries_within_thirty_seconds` |
| roll down: a failed delivery in the App's log is redelivered once asked (not one roll recorded, nor one that got through, nor again while its attempt is pending); with nothing in the log the reconcile ingests a direct push to main that stopped the mirror (merged in, mirror resumes) and a PR opened meanwhile, and a second look finds nothing | `github.rs::what_github_did_while_roll_was_down_is_caught_up` |

## Webhooks and commit statuses (`code-control/tests/webhooks.rs`)

| Behaviour | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| create validation (url scheme and credentials, unknown/empty events, short secret, `active`), generated secret shown once, `"*"`, list/get/patch/delete | `webhooks.rs::webhooks_deliver_signed_events` |
| signed ping on creation and on request; `landed`, `change`, `work`, `review` (once subscribed) deliveries with the payload shape; events not subscribed aren't sent; inactive hooks get nothing; a caller-chosen secret signs | `webhooks.rs::webhooks_deliver_signed_events` |
| a failing receiver retried until it answers (attempts in the log), redelivery, unknown delivery 404 | `webhooks.rs::webhooks_deliver_signed_events` |
| `push` events from git pushes | `webhooks.rs::pushes_are_webhook_events` |
| writers only (401 anonymous, 403 other owners) for hooks and statuses; private addresses refused at delivery with an admin token | `webhooks.rs::webhooks_need_writers_and_public_addresses` |
| `landed` from the durable land log: a 4,500-landing burst (more than the broadcast channel holds) delivered exactly once each; landings while webhooks weren't running delivered after a restart, from the persisted cursor; a new webhook gets no history | `webhooks.rs::landed_deliveries_survive_a_burst_larger_than_the_broadcast_channel`, `webhooks.rs::landings_while_the_daemon_was_down_are_delivered_after_a_restart`, `webhooks.rs::a_new_webhook_gets_no_history`, `code-store/tests/land_log.rs` |
| leaked roll tokens: what lands is read after it lands, from the land log with its own cursor (starting at the present); files that may hold a roll token go to roll-access with where they are, others don't (docs/permissions.md, "Leaked tokens") | `webhooks.rs::landed_files_are_scanned_for_roll_tokens` |
| the delivery log keeps the start of each response (2 KB, cut at a character) | `webhooks.rs::deliveries_keep_the_start_of_the_response` |
| two nodes forwarding one land log at once (their background forwarders and four more racing rounds) while landings arrive: each landing queued and delivered exactly once per webhook | `webhooks.rs::two_forwarders_deliver_each_landing_once` |
| a forwarder failing after queueing a page (a crash mid-page) keeps nothing of it and leaves the cursor; earlier pages stay forwarded, the next round forwards the rest, none twice | `webhooks.rs::a_forwarder_failing_mid_page_neither_drops_nor_duplicates` |
| a webhook created, changed or deleted through one node applies at once to events emitted on another (no per-node cache) | `webhooks.rs::webhooks_made_on_one_node_apply_to_events_on_another` |
| webhooks created through two nodes while the forwarder starts for the first time (no cursor yet) all succeed, and each gets the next landing once | `webhooks.rs::webhooks_created_while_the_forwarder_starts` |
| two nodes' delivery workers on one queue, every first attempt failing: each delivery sent exactly twice (the failure and the retry), both attempts in the log | `webhooks.rs::two_delivery_workers_send_each_delivery_once_including_retries` |
| a delivery claimed by a node that died is sent by another node once the claim lapses, not before, and once | `webhooks.rs::a_claim_held_by_a_dead_node_is_retried_after_it_lapses` |
| a receiver slower than the claim: the request times out inside the claim and is recorded; the retry is the only other send | `webhooks.rs::a_slow_receiver_never_gets_an_unrecorded_second_send` |
| failing statuses on the default branch: one `broken-main` item per context (deduplicated), a certain culprit reverted (and landed with `auto_revert`), an uncertain one named as a range, closed by a later success for that context only, not by an older one; statuses off the default branch break nothing | `webhooks.rs::failing_statuses_on_the_default_branch_break_main`, `webhooks.rs::failing_statuses_auto_revert` |
| delivery workers start with webhooks: a delivery left queued by a stopped process goes out without waiting for a new event; a stopped process's tasks don't keep its `Code` alive | `webhooks.rs::deliveries_left_queued_are_sent_after_a_restart`, `webhooks.rs::landings_while_the_daemon_was_down_are_delivered_after_a_restart` |
| events are queued for webhooks before `emit` returns; the main state changes (a change opened, queued, closed and landed, a review, a check, a commit status) promise their event in their own transaction (`webhook_outbox`): a node dying right after the commit loses nothing, and the land forwarder's node delivers each promise once, with the change or review as it is then; kept promises leave nothing behind | `webhooks.rs::events_promised_by_a_state_change_survive_a_crash_after_its_commit` |
| statuses: pending/error/success → checks on the changes at that head (`source: external`, URL, `error:` summary); a queue repo's `required_checks` unblocked by external CI; combined state over contexts; a commit with no change recorded only; by git commit (and prefix) after a push; validation (state, target_url, context length), unknown commit 404; `check` events; deleting the repo removes hooks and statuses | `webhooks.rs::commit_statuses_become_checks`, `webhooks.rs::pushes_are_webhook_events` |

## Cluster (`code-control/tests/cluster.rs`)

Three nodes in one process, each a `Code` with its own server, sharing one database pair (two
fresh Postgres schemas with `ROLL_CODE_TEST_DATABASE`, else one SQLite pair) and one object
directory, with `ClusterConfig::for_tests` timings (docs/code/cluster.md). Leases are in code's
database, or on DynamoDB Local with `ROLL_CODE_TEST_CLUSTER_BACKEND=dynamodb`: `pnpm test:code
--docker` runs the whole file both ways (data on Postgres).

| Behaviour | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| which routes are repo-scoped (repos, git, changes, threads, work items) and which are global | `code-control/src/cluster.rs::repo_scoped_paths_are_classified` |
| every repo request through every node runs on the repo's owner: API reads and landings, changes, threads and work items by id, git push and clone through other nodes, an event stream through a non-owner (with an event emitted on a third node, forwarded to the owner), MCP tool calls | `cluster.rs::every_request_for_a_repo_runs_on_its_owner` |
| 64 concurrent landings over every node: each lands exactly once | `cluster.rs::concurrent_landings_through_every_node_land_exactly_once` |
| a killed owner (port closed, connections dropped, no `leave`): another node takes the repo after the lease expires (not before), landings through the others keep going, every acknowledged one is on main once | `cluster.rs::a_dead_owners_repo_moves_and_keeps_every_acknowledged_landing` |
| the merge queue: a dead owner's in-flight batch is recovered, tested again and landed by the new owner | `cluster.rs::the_merge_queue_continues_a_dead_owners_batch` |
| roll's reviewer: a review running on a dead owner is queued again and run by the new owner (2 attempts) | `cluster.rs::a_running_review_runs_again_after_its_owner_dies` |
| roll's reviewer: restarting another node (its reviewer starting again) leaves a running review alone: one model call, one attempt, metered once | `cluster.rs::restarting_a_node_doesnt_run_another_nodes_review_again` |
| webhooks of landings: the `land-forwarder` singleton's node dies; it forwards nothing more, a survivor takes the singleton and every landing is delivered in exactly one delivery | `cluster.rs::the_land_forwarder_moves_with_its_singleton` |
| a joining node gets its share of the repos (each moves to its preferred node) while landings and reads through every node never fail | `cluster.rs::a_joining_node_takes_repos_over_without_failing_requests` |
| draining (SIGTERM) moves every repo off the node before it leaves, the health check fails meanwhile, and requests through the others never fail | `cluster.rs::draining_moves_every_repo_off_a_node_before_it_leaves` |
| a rename keeps the repo on its owner; the old name answers 301 and git follows it, through any node | `cluster.rs::every_request_for_a_repo_runs_on_its_owner` |
| an event stream ends as soon as its repo starts moving, without holding the move up | `cluster.rs::an_event_stream_ends_when_its_repo_moves` |
| an owner's agents run on the holder of `agents:<owner id>`: a machine's link through another node lands there, commands and event streams through a third node reach it; when the lease moves the link closes with 1012 and reconnects to the new holder, resuming from the mirror | `cluster.rs::an_owners_agents_follow_their_lease`, `code-control/src/cluster.rs::repo_scoped_paths_are_classified` |
| health: 200 with the node's id while it holds its leases, 503 while it can't reach the database, 200 again after | `cluster.rs::health_follows_each_node` |
| a landing on one node reaches a webhook within well under the 5 s poll when the forwarder runs on another node (Postgres `NOTIFY` in the landing's transaction; in-process on SQLite) | `cluster.rs::landings_on_one_node_wake_the_forwarder_on_another`, `code-sql/tests/backends.rs::listen_wakes_on_notify`, `code-sql/tests/backends.rs::listen_wakes_on_notify_after_commit_in_one_process` |
| losing a repo drops its code index; deleting one (through a non-owner) removes the owner's index and deletes its lease row, which nobody takes back; a 404 takes no lease | `cluster.rs::moving_or_deleting_a_repo_leaves_no_index_or_lease_behind` |
| an idle repo (no request, stream or work for `repo_idle_release`, no work waiting) is given up and its row deleted; an open stream or waiting work keeps it; the next request takes it again | `cluster.rs::idle_repos_are_released_and_taken_again_on_demand`, `roll-cluster/tests/core.rs::idle_keys_are_released_and_reacquired_on_demand`, `roll-cluster/tests/core.rs::a_retired_key_loses_its_lease_row_and_comes_back_only_on_demand` |
| indexes left on disk: a single node removes those of deleted repos at startup, a cluster node all of them | `cluster.rs::leftover_indexes_are_removed_at_startup` |
| GitHub deliveries for a repo another node owns are recorded before GitHub is answered: the owner dying mid-delivery, or unreachable when one arrives, gets 202 and the next owner handles each part once; redeliveries don't run twice | `cluster.rs::github_deliveries_survive_their_owner_dying` |
| WebSocket-style upgrades through a non-owner reach the owner and stream both ways (3 MiB echoed); reads whose connection drops before an answer are sent again, writes never | `roll-cluster/tests/forward.rs::upgrades_are_forwarded_and_stream_both_ways`, `roll-cluster/tests/forward.rs::reads_are_sent_again_when_the_connection_drops_before_an_answer` |

### The cluster layer (`roll-cluster`)

`roll-cluster/tests/suite.rs` runs on every backend: in-process (`memory::`), `code_nodes`/
`code_leases` on SQLite or, with `ROLL_CODE_TEST_DATABASE`, Postgres (`postgres::`), and a fresh
table on DynamoDB Local in Docker (`dynamodb::`, container `roll-cloud-cluster-ddb`; skipped with
a message without Docker). The rest is backend-independent and runs in-process.

| Behaviour | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| every node routes a key to one owner, its preferred node, which takes it on first use; nothing moves while membership is stable | `roll-cluster/tests/suite.rs::every_node_routes_a_key_to_the_same_owner` |
| contenders racing for one lease: exactly one wins, the epoch rises by one per new holder | `roll-cluster/tests/suite.rs::exactly_one_contender_wins` |
| a dead node's keys move to their preferred live nodes with a new epoch, never while it could still act on them | `roll-cluster/tests/suite.rs::a_dead_nodes_keys_move_to_their_preferred_live_node` |
| self-fencing: a node cut off from the backend emits `Lost` and fails health before anyone takes its key, and gets it back after | `roll-cluster/tests/suite.rs::a_node_cut_off_from_the_backend_fences_itself_first_and_recovers` |
| draining hands every key to its new preferred node at once (not after a lease's length) | `roll-cluster/tests/suite.rs::draining_hands_everything_over` |
| a pinned node id waits for its previous incarnation; the replaced process owns nothing | `roll-cluster/tests/suite.rs::a_pinned_node_id_waits_for_its_previous_incarnation` |
| a joining node gets its keys by handoff; a guard holds a handoff back until dropped, or until the handoff timeout | `roll-cluster/tests/core.rs::a_joining_node_gets_its_keys_by_handoff_and_a_guard_holds_one_back`, `roll-cluster/tests/core.rs::a_handoff_goes_ahead_after_its_timeout` |
| a singleton has one holder at a time and moves when it dies | `roll-cluster/tests/core.rs::a_singleton_has_one_holder_and_moves_when_it_dies` |
| leaving releases everything at once; an outage shorter than the margin loses nothing | `roll-cluster/tests/core.rs::leaving_releases_everything`, `roll-cluster/tests/core.rs::a_blip_shorter_than_the_lease_margin_loses_nothing` |
| forwarding: methods, headers, queries, big bodies and streamed answers both ways, one retry after Unavailable, the hop limit, signatures | `roll-cluster/tests/forward.rs::forwards_methods_headers_and_queries_to_the_owner`, `roll-cluster/tests/forward.rs::streams_large_bodies_both_ways`, `roll-cluster/tests/forward.rs::streams_responses_incrementally`, `roll-cluster/tests/forward.rs::retries_once_after_the_owner_answers_unavailable`, `roll-cluster/tests/forward.rs::refuses_to_forward_past_the_hop_limit`, `roll-cluster/tests/forward.rs::rejects_unsigned_and_tampered_internal_requests` |
| rendezvous placement spreads keys and moves about 1/N when a node joins | `roll-cluster/tests/placement.rs::rendezvous_spreads_keys_and_moves_about_one_in_n` |
| rolld's cluster settings: the backend follows the secret, roll code's flags are aliases, a cluster needs a secret | `rolld/src/cluster.rs::secrets_pick_the_backend_and_code_flags_are_aliases` |

### Cluster in Docker (`rolld/tests/code_cluster_e2e.rs`)

Three real `rolld --code-only` containers from `deploy/code/Dockerfile` (dev profile) on a private
network, with Postgres 18 and SeaweedFS (S3), production cluster timings, an admin token and a
webhook receiver container. Runs in `pnpm test:code --docker` (`ROLL_E2E=1`); skipped
otherwise. The module docs give the client retry policy and exactly what is asserted.

| Behaviour | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| under continuous traffic through every node (API landings on four repos, pushes to `refs/land/main`, clones, fetches, reads): SIGKILL of the busiest repo's owner, a rolling restart (SIGTERM: health 503 while draining, exit 0 within the drain timeout, no leases left, healthy again on start) and a Postgres restart (every node healthy again without restarting). Every acknowledged landing (API or push) is on its branch exactly once (changeset ids, messages, and each operation's file added by one first-parent commit); nothing landed twice or unsent; the first-parent history over the git protocol is the API's log; a clone through each node has the same head; every landing after a webhook's creation reaches it under exactly one delivery id, signed, repeated only for attempts sent in a disruption window; health 200 on every node; no client operation fails after the retry policy. Prints the timeline (kill, lease moved, served again, drain time, Postgres recovery) and per-kind error counts | `rolld/tests/code_cluster_e2e.rs::code_cluster_keeps_every_landing_through_kill_rolling_restart_and_postgres_restart` |

| a commit whose reply was lost (`CommitUnknown`) is resolved before answering: committed is acknowledged and published once, rolled back fails and lands again once (batches, ref updates, `advance`); the decision from `pg_xact_status` or the ref | `lander.rs::a_lost_commit_reply_is_resolved_before_answering`, `lander::tests::the_transaction_status_settles_a_lost_commit`, `lander::tests::without_it_the_ref_decides` |
