## control.db on SQLite and Postgres (`code-control/tests/concurrency.rs`, `code-sql/tests/backends.rs`)

Every code-control test runs on SQLite, and on Postgres with `ROLL_CODE_TEST_DATABASE` set
(control.db in a fresh schema; a restart in a test reopens the same one).

| Behaviour | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| two nodes on one control database (the same SQLite file, or the same Postgres schema through their own connections): change and work item numbers created at once from both are each used exactly once | `concurrency.rs::numbers_are_unique_across_nodes` |
| agents claiming at once through both nodes' APIs: each work item goes to exactly one claimant | `concurrency.rs::each_work_item_has_exactly_one_claimant` |
| merge queue entries made at once on both nodes get distinct positions; a change enqueued on both at once has one entry | `concurrency.rs::queue_positions_are_unique_across_nodes` |
| tokens made at once on both nodes all work on both; a revoked one stops working on the other node at once | `concurrency.rs::tokens_work_and_revoke_across_nodes` |
| MCP sessions in control.db: one started through a node works through another and after a restart, ends everywhere when ended on one, lapses when idle; `last_seen` written only when a few seconds old | `mcp.rs::sessions_work_through_any_node` |
| text with a NUL character is a 400 on Postgres (which can't store it), leaving nothing half-written; SQLite keeps it | `api.rs::nul_in_text_is_a_400_on_postgres` |
| one SQL dialect on both backends: types, byte-order text, upserts, serializable re-run writes, rollbacks, migrations, columns named like types | `code-sql/tests/backends.rs::writes_are_serializable_and_retried`, `code-sql/tests/backends.rs::failures_roll_back_and_are_never_reported_as_success`, `code-sql/tests/backends.rs::schema_migrations_and_columns`, `code-sql/tests/backends.rs::columns_named_like_types` |

## Security regressions

| Defect fixed | Test |
| --- | --- |
| control planes without auth: every route rolld merges answers 401 without the operator token (and to a wrong one, or another credential, where the product checks its own) and not 401 with it, except `crates/rolld/src/auth.rs`'s public list; CORS answers only `ROLL_DASHBOARD_ORIGIN` | `rolld/tests/auth.rs::every_control_plane_route_needs_the_operator_token` |
| rolld off loopback (or `ROLL_EDGE=1`) without `ROLL_ADMIN_TOKEN`, or with a short one, refuses to start and doesn't print it | `rolld/tests/auth.rs::refuses_to_listen_off_loopback_without_a_token` |
| owners' provider `base_url` on private addresses (SSRF; literals at set and use time, credentials in the URL) | `reviewer.rs::byok_endpoints_on_private_addresses_are_refused` |
| provider answers over 16 MiB, and redirects | `code-review/tests/engine.rs::oversized_answers_and_redirects_are_refused` |
| NAT64/6to4-wrapped private IPv4, `*.localhost` | `review::apps` unit `private_ranges` |
| approvals or change requests from GitHub users who can't push counted | `github.rs::reviews_both_ways` (author_association cases) |
| roll approving after skipping files, or incrementally with its findings open | `reviewer.rs::roll_doesnt_approve_what_it_didnt_read`, `code-review` unit `approvals_need_everything_read` |
| exponential `**` matching (search `path`, CODEOWNERS) | `code-review` unit `glob::many_double_stars_stay_fast`, `review::pattern` unit `many_double_stars_stay_fast` |
| custom reviewer prompt shown to public readers | `reviewer.rs::custom_prompt_is_for_writers` |
| markdown links with `\` or control characters (`/\evil.dev`) | `markdown.test.ts` "drops unsafe link targets" |
| last 4 characters of very short keys | `code-review` unit `secret::round_trip_and_binding` |
| review apps approving or requesting changes on other owners' public repos | `review.rs::review_apps_events_scopes_requests_and_failed_deliveries` |
| a secret shown once (owner token, review-app credentials, webhook secret) shown again after navigating back to a page kept in `<Activity>` | `connect-agent.test.tsx`, `owner.test.tsx`, `webhooks.test.tsx` (dashboard) |
| agent tokens drove everything on a machine: terminals running any command, `fs.list /`, approving their own requests (`accept_for_session`), adding projects, full_access threads | `code-control/src/agents/policy.rs::agents_drive_threads_but_never_reach_past_them`, `routes.rs::agents_routes_follow_the_auth_matrix`, `mcp.rs::agent_tools_drive_a_machine_like_their_routes`, `mcp.rs::agent_tools_work_in_roll_code_like_their_routes` |
| public shares kept a project's remote with its credentials and its private repo, homes after a scheme or a flag (`file:///home/me`, `-I/home/me`), and tokens in tool inputs and outputs | `agents.rs::shares_freeze_a_redacted_copy_for_anyone_with_the_link`, `code-control/src/agents/share.rs::credentials_are_redacted`, `code-control/src/agents/share.rs::home_directories_become_tilde` |
| a publish grant pushed anything in its repo: `main`, a tag `v9`, another branch | `routes.rs::agent_grants_follow_the_auth_matrix` (real git: main, another branch, a tag, an atomic push and a delete refused; the thread's branch pushed; on main a 400) |
| 10,000 anonymous `POST /v1/code/agents/login`s blocked every login for 15 minutes | `agents.rs::logins_are_capped_per_client_and_a_full_table_never_locks_others_out` |
| one daemon frame the relay couldn't parse closed the link (4400), and the replies of commands in flight were lost | `agents.rs::unreadable_daemon_frames_are_skipped_not_the_link` |
| a machine could fill control.db with records without bound | `agents.rs::the_mirror_keeps_a_machine_within_its_quota`, `agent-host/tests/relay.rs::unreadable_frames_are_skipped` (the notice reaches `roll agent up`) |
| a create whose answer was lost could leave a paid instance (with a machine token in it) that no sandbox stood for | `sandboxes.rs::the_sweep_deletes_instances_no_sandbox_stands_for` |
| docker sandboxes shared the default bridge (they could reach each other) and `disk_gb` wasn't applied | `sandboxes.rs::the_docker_backend_runs_hardened_containers` (real Docker: the dedicated network, ICC off), `code-control/src/agents/sandbox/docker.rs::run_args_are_hardened_and_carry_no_secrets` |

## Other regressions

Defects found by the phase-3 coverage audit, each with the test that failed before its fix.

| Defect fixed | Test |
| --- | --- |
| a finished change's preview was removed twice when a late check report re-sent its landed state | `code-preview/tests/service.rs::previews_follow_their_change` |
| a stopped process's delivery worker kept its `Code` alive (and its forwarder running) for up to a minute | `webhooks.rs::landings_while_the_daemon_was_down_are_delivered_after_a_restart` |
| deliveries left queued by a stopped process waited for a new event before anything sent them | `webhooks.rs::deliveries_left_queued_are_sent_after_a_restart` |
| concurrent public reviews both priced free against the last free review, then one was charged past `monthly_cap_usd` | `reviewer.rs::concurrent_public_reviews_on_two_nodes_never_share_the_last_free_one` |
| a failing review ran four times instead of three | `reviewer.rs::provider_errors_403_persistent_529_and_400` |
| a failed `@roll` chat left the thread without an answer | `reviewer.rs::provider_errors_403_persistent_529_and_400` |
| acknowledged landings could be lost on an OS crash or power loss (WAL not fsynced) | `lander.rs::landings_are_synced_before_they_are_acknowledged_and_checkpointed_in_the_background` |
| a PR closed (or merged) on GitHub stayed in the merge queue and could land | `github.rs::pull_request_issue_and_installation_actions` |
| GitHub draft PRs weren't drafts; `ready_for_review` and `converted_to_draft` were ignored | `github.rs::pull_request_issue_and_installation_actions`, `code-github/tests/events.rs::every_pull_request_action` |
| a required check going red while its batch was tested didn't keep the change off main | `queue.rs::a_check_going_red_during_the_test_keeps_the_change_off_main` |
| no `queue` event ever carried state `landing` | `queue.rs::pausing_lets_the_batch_under_test_finish` |
| a disabled or deleted required review app blocked changes forever | `review.rs::review_apps_events_scopes_requests_and_failed_deliveries` |
| bad query strings answered plain text instead of a JSON 400 | `routes.rs::statuses_of_every_route`, `reviewer.rs::repo_reviewer_routes_answer_every_status` |
| anonymous owner create, owner patch and token create answered 403, so the dashboard never asked to sign in | `routes.rs::every_repo_route_follows_the_auth_matrix`, `api.rs::repos_rename_and_visibility` |
| MCP `comment` hid "not found" behind "needs a thread"; `create_change` refused work items by number | `mcp.rs::tool_argument_errors`, `mcp.rs::tools_map_onto_their_routes` |
| `roll code` exited 2 (rebase and retry) for usage errors and for landing a landed or closed change | `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::repo_land_and_usage_edges`, `roll-cli/tests/code.rs::change_and_work_edges` |
| the MCP snippet lost its auth header placeholder before a token was created | `connect-agent.test.tsx` (dashboard) |
| a thread resolved on GitHub could be resolved back there when a notification read the thread between "GitHub has it resolved" and the resolution itself (now one write, read in one statement) | `github.rs::reviews_both_ways` |

## Dashboard (`apps/dashboard`, vitest)

jsdom (`vitest.config.mts` projects `node` and `dom`, setup in `src/test/`).

| Area | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| automations: every `automation*.json` fixture decodes strictly and keeps every field, built-in step configs decode as their kind's, every trigger sampled; the client calls the routes; the stream's automation, automation_removed, automation_run and notice events | `src/lib/automations-api.test.ts`, `src/lib/agents-api.test.ts` |
| the automations demo (`?demo`): believable automations, a week of runs and a catalog that decode strictly as the relay's; a run starts a real thread on the demo canvas (in a worktree), waits for it and sends a notice; tests fill templates and judge conditions; cancel, retry, secret names only | `src/lib/automations-demo.test.ts`, `src/lib/automations-store.test.ts` (`automations demo`) |
| automations view logic: cron in words, trigger summaries, the `{{` under the caret and inserting a variable, the variables a step may read (event fields, the run, earlier steps only), validation where it's wrong (names, cron, conditions, required fields, duplicate ids, later steps, secrets outside a webhook's headers), the catalog with the built-ins filled in, sample events that meet the conditions (or the server's `sample`), every template building a valid spec, seven days of runs, the live last run, every `#agents/automations…` link round-tripping | `src/lib/automations-ui.test.ts` |
| automations store: the newer cursor wins between a load and the stream, a removed one stays removed, replays change nothing, threads indexed to the runs that started them, runs and notices off the client's stream without touching the canvas records | `src/lib/automations-store.test.ts` |
| the Automations view: rows with trigger, last run and seven days; pausing from the switch; the empty state's templates opening the builder; building one from nothing (a step from the catalog, found by typing in its searchable list and added with Enter, its schema form, a variable from `{{`, Test with verdicts on the cards and rendered values under fields, Create); inline problems block saving; a template pointing at the owner's machine; a run's timeline moving live on `automation_run`, its thread opening beside the canvas; cancel; the automation chip on a thread's canvas card opening its run | `src/components/agents/automations/automations.test.tsx` |
| the automations list's logic: a row's run going on and its last runs oldest first; search on every word across name, trigger in words and step kinds; Status (on, paused, last run failed, running now), Trigger and Project filters alone and together; filter values only where they occur, with counts | `src/lib/automations-list.test.ts` |
| the automations list in the sheet: search, the Filter popover (status, trigger) with chips, removing one and Clear; ↑/↓ between rows; a row's hover actions (Run now opens the run, Duplicate a paused copy, Edit the builder, Pause); the marks of integrations its steps use; its last runs as bars | `src/components/agents/automations/automations.test.tsx` ("shows each automation's trigger…") |
| the automations sheet's stack: each view's level (Build and Settings one level, the list's tabs one level), a deep link's parents keeping the list's tab, Back to where you came from (dropping what came between), a saved new automation replaced by its overview, which way each page slides, every level's `#agents/automations…` link round-tripping (the conversation under its run too) | `src/lib/automations-nav.test.ts` |

## Desktop app (`apps/desktop`, vitest)

Main-process logic in node, without Electron (`pnpm --filter @roll/desktop test`); the daemon and
login tests drive a fake `roll` process (`apps/desktop/test/fake-roll.mjs`).

| Behaviour | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| navigation: the app's origin (and configured extras) stay in the window; other http(s) and mailto go to the system browser; new windows never open; file/javascript/data/custom schemes and credentialed URLs are dropped | `src/main/policy.test.ts` (`navigation policy`) |
| permissions: only notifications, sanitized clipboard writes and fullscreen, only for the app's top frame | `src/main/policy.test.ts` (`permissions`) |
| deep links: `roll://agents`, `…/thread/<id>`, `…/new`, `…/connect?code=`; everything else (other hosts, extra segments, encoded traversal, bad codes, credentials, overlong) refused; dashboard paths; links in a second instance's argv | `src/main/policy.test.ts` (`deep links`) |
| two windows: agents, thread and new links go to the Agents window, connect links to the main window; the Agents window opens on `/dashboard/agents`, `?focus=t:<id>` or `?new`; an open one gets the link sent (listening page), queued (loading page) or loaded (a page that doesn't listen); `openAgents` targets from a page parsed exactly; the preload's `window` from its argument | `src/main/windows.test.ts` |
| settings: defaults written 0600 on first run (roll.sh/dashboard, api.roll.sh), the old default (the canvas) moved to the dashboard, self-hosted origin as API, `ROLL_DESKTOP_URL`/`ROLL_DESKTOP_API` overrides, bad fields ignored with warnings, a corrupt file never overwritten | `src/main/settings.test.ts` (`settings`) |
| window state: each window's bounds (the Agents window's under `agents`; an older state.json keeps the main window's) and paused round-trip; bounds restored only on a connected display with the title bar reachable, clamped to the minimum | `src/main/settings.test.ts` (`window state`) |
| this machine: the CLI from `ROLL_BIN`, `resources/bin` (packaged) or `target/{release,debug}`; agent.json where the CLI keeps it (`ROLL_AGENT_CONFIG`, `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`); signed-in check without reading the token; PATH from a noisy login shell | `src/main/settings.test.ts` (`this machine`) |
| daemon supervisor: crash loop restarts at 40/80/160/160 ms (doubling, capped), backoff resets after a long run, SIGTERM stop with output logged, SIGKILL after the grace period, stop cancels a pending restart, idempotent start, missing binary retried; log rotation | `src/main/supervisor.test.ts` |
| status from the page: parsed and bounded (counts, lengths, ids); each pending request notifies once and the backlog at launch doesn't; notification wording links to the thread | `src/main/status.test.ts` (`status from the page`) |
| tray and menus: every machine state (connected from the daemon's log or the page, connecting, reconnecting, retrying countdown, offline, paused, signed out, signing in with code, CLI missing, managed outside), running and waiting counts, pause/resume/connect enabled only when they apply; "Open agents" and "Open roll" in the tray and app menu; app menu roles and shortcuts per platform | `src/main/status.test.ts` (`tray`, `daemon output`) |
| first-run login: the code and approve URL from `--json` or text output, bounded line reading, `roll agent login --json --api` run to approval (agent.json written), denial reported | `src/main/status.test.ts` (`first-run login`) |
| bridge types identical in the desktop app and the dashboard | `src/main/bridge-parity.test.ts` |

| worktree cleanup (T3's `worktreeSettledAfterDays`): a settled thread's worktree goes after `cleanup.worktree_after_days`, not before; its branch keeps unpushed commits and its checkpoints stay; kept: local changes, another branch checked out, a running terminal, not settled; diffs and `git.status` still answer, after a restart too; the next message puts it back on its branch in the same place | `agent-host/tests/threads.rs::settled_worktrees_are_cleaned_up_without_losing_anything_and_come_back` |
| `project.cleanup` now: removes archived and settled threads' clean worktrees and reports the kept ones (`local_changes` for an ignored `.env` under `any_local_files`, `shared` for a fork working in its parent's folder); under `uncommitted_changes` an ignored file doesn't keep it; a terminal or a rollback puts a worktree back; days over 3650 refused | `agent-host/tests/threads.rs::cleaning_up_now_says_what_it_kept_and_a_terminal_or_rollback_brings_a_worktree_back`, `agent-host/tests/orchestrator.rs` (project settings) |
| idle provider sessions close after 30 minutes (T3's `idle_timeout`), not before; the next message resumes in a new session | `agent-host/tests/threads.rs::idle_provider_sessions_close_after_half_an_hour` |
| deleting a thread keeps a worktree with uncommitted changes (and the branch) | `agent-host/tests/threads.rs::worktree_threads_get_a_branch_and_delete_leaves_only_tombstones_and_the_branch` |
| terminals: settling closes the thread's finished ones (a running one stays); archiving closes them all (T3) | `agent-host/tests/threads.rs::settling_closes_finished_terminals_and_archiving_closes_them_all` |
| cleanup on the wire: `ProjectSettings.cleanup`, `AutoSettle.keep_changes`, `Worktree.removed_at`, `project.cleanup` → `Reply::Cleaned` with every `KeptReason`; records from before parse, cleanup off leaves no trace | `agent-proto/tests/fixtures.rs::records_from_before_settling_parse`, fixtures |
| context window: each `ProviderEvent::Context` goes on the run and the thread (between runs on the thread alone); a fork shows its parent's at the fork point and clears it when it starts fresh with a summary; a native rollback shows the kept run's; a provider switch shows that provider's conversation's (none without one) | `agent-host/tests/threads.rs::the_context_window_follows_the_conversation` |
| context usage on the wire: `Thread.context`, `Run.context`; records from before it parse; a percentage only with a window | `agent-proto/tests/fixtures.rs::records_from_before_settling_parse`, `agent-proto/tests/fixtures.rs::context_percent_needs_a_window` |
| usage limits (T3's `ServerProviderUsageLimits`): `ProviderInfo.usage_limits` on the wire; windows sorted by kind, percentages clamped; an update replaces its window and keeps the reset time and length it leaves out, a repeat changes nothing; a probe keeps newer streamed windows and an empty probe keeps the last; when every exhausted window resets | `agent-proto/tests/usage_limits.rs`, fixtures |
| usage limits from sessions: `ProviderEvent::UsageLimits` during a turn and between turns goes on the provider's info and `hello`; a probe without limits keeps them | `agent-host/tests/orchestrator.rs::session_usage_limits_go_on_the_providers_info` |

## Agents end to end (`pnpm test:agents`, `scripts/agents-e2e.mjs`)

The real `rolld --code-only` and `roll` on free ports with a throwaway home; the daemon offers
only the scripted fake providers (`ROLL_AGENT_FAKE=1`).

| Step | Checked |
| --- | --- |
| `roll agent login` | the pending lookup shows its name, hostname and OS; approved through the API; `agent.json` saved |
| `roll agent up` | online with the `fake`, `echo` and `orchestrate` providers |
| project add, thread create | replies `created`; the thread reaches the snapshot |
| a streamed run | text deltas on `/events?threads=…&after=…`, the approval request pending, nothing written before it's answered |
| the approval answered | the run completes with a checkpoint (stat 1 file, +1), the checkpoint record, the file in the project |
| `diff.get` of the run | `ROLL_DEMO.md` added with the prompt |
| a reconnect with `?after=` and a newer `Last-Event-ID` | resumes after the newer |
| a share | the link on the web app, read anonymously (`no-store`), every record kind, the edit, no home directory |
| "Orchestrate" (`thread.create {orchestrator: true, provider: "orchestrate"}`) | over `/events` (60 s bound): 2 subagent threads under the orchestrator (`orchestrate`, not orchestrators), their runs and `subagent` nodes completed, a completed wake run, the orchestrator idle with `All 2 parts done.` |
| the dashboard (`vite build`, `node server.mjs`) | `/dashboard/agents` 200 with its title; the share page 200 titled after the thread, OpenGraph and `noindex`; its preview image a PNG; a missing share 404 |
| `roll agent logout` | `up` stops (exit 1, "roll agent login"), the machine is gone, the share stays |
| `install.sh` (`scripts/test-agents.sh`) | from a local release: this machine's asset, checksum checked, installed and runs; a wrong checksum installs nothing |

## Agents (providers)

Each adapter runs against a fake provider process that replays recorded protocol transcripts;
scenario events are compared with golden files (Codex; `ROLL_FIXTURES=write` regenerates them) or
with the expected events written in the test (Claude).

| Context window: the main agent's message usage (input, cache writes and reads, output) from `message_start`, `message_delta` and the snapshot, reported once per change; the window from T3's model manifest (`[1m]`, dated ids); `post_tokens` after a compaction | `agent-host/tests/claude.rs::real_cli_streams_text_and_reasoning`, `agent-host/tests/claude.rs::todos_edits_and_notices`, `agent-host/src/provider/claude/models.rs::context_windows_follow_t3s_manifest` |
| Error results fail the turn with Claude's reason | `agent-host/tests/claude.rs::error_results_fail_the_turn` |
| `info()`: version, models for that version (legacy ones after the current ones) and the current ones it needs an update for, login from the `initialize` account (no model call); not installed; each model's options from T3's manifest profiles (reasoning defaults, Fast Mode, context window, thinking), a custom slug of a known family getting its family's | `agent-host/tests/claude.rs::info_reports_version_login_and_models`, `agent-host/src/provider/claude.rs::auth_follows_the_account`, `agent-host/src/provider/claude/models.rs::models_follow_the_installed_version`, `agent-host/src/provider/claude/models.rs::options_follow_t3s_profiles` |
| model options on threads: `thread.create` and `thread.configure` carry them to the session; a new model keeps the ones it takes; a change of options alone starts the next turn in a session that has them, the same model and options keep the live one; options are checked (count, size, never the effort); Codex's service tier on `turn/start`; ACP agents' own config options of the same id; options as the wire describes them (an older daemon's `efforts` read as the reasoning option) | `agent-host/tests/threads.rs::model_options_reach_the_session_and_follow_the_model`, `agent-host/tests/codex.rs::mcp_servers_instructions_and_images_reach_codex`, `agent-host/src/provider/acp/policy.rs::options_go_to_the_agents_own_config_options`, `agent-proto/tests/models.rs` |
| Robustness: a crash fails the turn and exits with the stderr tail; bounded lines; image and PDF attachments only | `agent-host/tests/claude.rs::crash_fails_the_turn_with_stderr`, `agent-host/tests/claude.rs::launch_flags_follow_the_session_config`, `agent-host/src/provider/claude.rs::long_lines_are_skipped` |

### OpenCode (`agent-host/tests/opencode.rs`, transcripts in `agent-host/tests/fixtures/opencode/`)

T3 Code's OpenCode 2.0.18 replay fixtures (MIT, `opencode2-http.sse`), ported to roll (each file's
`metadata.roll` says what changed), and transcripts written in the tests. The test binary is the
fake `opencode serve` too: it answers HTTP and streams events from the transcript with T3's
matching rules (subset match, `<any>`, roll's prompt ids bound to the recorded ones, a request
waits until earlier recorded events went out) and checks Basic auth and the server's environment.

| Feature | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| The shared server: `opencode serve --hostname=… --port=…`, the listening line, `/api/info`, roll's own password (an inherited `OPENCODE_SERVER_PASSWORD` dropped), `OPENCODE_CONFIG_CONTENT` `{}`, the event stream subscribed first | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::status_lists_models_and_never_their_settings` |
| A port taken between picking it and the server binding it: the server exits before it listens and starts again on another (three tries) | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::a_port_taken_before_the_server_binds_it_gets_another` |
| A session for full access (location, model and variant, rules), roll's instructions entry, a prompt with roll's id, streamed text and reasoning, usage summed over steps, the context window from the last step | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::simple` |
| Tools by name (shell → command with exit code, edit/write → file change, glob/grep → search, read without its envelope, todowrite → todos, roll's MCP tools) | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::permission`, `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::subagent`, `agent-host/src/provider/opencode/tools.rs::tools_map_by_name`, `agent-host/src/provider/opencode/tools.rs::read_envelopes_come_off` |
| Permission asks → approvals: accept once, decline steers T3's note in then rejects with a message; supervised rules with the agent's path allows | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::permission`, `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::resume_registers_mcp_and_switches_modes` |
| Forms → questions, answers back as option values; unsupported fields refused | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::question`, `agent-host/src/provider/opencode/translate.rs::form_answers_map_labels_to_values` |
| Asks that come with no turn are refused; an execution OpenCode left running is stopped before the next turn starts | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::an_execution_left_running_stops_before_the_next_turn` |
| `interrupt`: the running tool interrupted, the turn ends interrupted | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::interrupt` |
| Native steering (`delivery: "steer"`, roll's steer ids); an undelivered steer holds the turn's end | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::inbox_steer`, `agent-host/src/provider/opencode/translate.rs::a_steer_not_yet_delivered_holds_the_turn` |
| `/compact` → `session.compact` with the turn's id; the compaction row with the context before it | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::compaction` |
| Fork before the next turn's prompt (history read once, paginated), the copy's rules, a turn on the copy | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::fork` |
| Rollback: `revert/stage` at the first dropped turn's prompt (history only), then `revert/commit` | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::revert` |
| A workspace command (`/hello WORLD` → `session.command`), named by the message OpenCode made of it | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::command`, `agent-host/src/provider/opencode/session.rs::commands_parse` |
| Subagents: the `subagent` call, its child session's work beneath it (scoped ids), its result without the wrapper, the child's model | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::subagent` |
| Background subagents folded into their turn: the turn ends once OpenCode delivered the report | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::background_subagent_folds_into_its_turn` |
| Resume (`GET` the session, its rules brought to the modes, moved to the thread's folder), roll's MCP servers registered per session (and only theirs allowed) and removed on close, plan mode as the plan agent, the effort as the model's variant | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::resume_registers_mcp_and_switches_modes` |
| `info()`: version, the catalog (variants as reasoning with T3's defaults, legacy releases, images), a model's `settings` never read or shown; no models → run `opencode auth login`; 1.x left to the ACP agent | `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::status_lists_models_and_never_their_settings`, `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::status_without_models_asks_for_a_login`, `agent-host/tests/opencode.rs::opencode_1x_is_left_to_the_acp_agent`, `agent-host/src/provider/opencode.rs::catalog_follows_t3`, `agent-host/src/provider/opencode/wire.rs::model_entries_never_keep_settings` |

### Muse Code (`agent-host/tests/muse.rs`, transcripts in `agent-host/tests/fixtures/muse/`)

T3 Code's Muse replay fixtures (MIT, `muse.msp-jsonl`, recorded from Muse 1.4.3–1.4.6): `simple`,
`turn_interrupt`, `subagent_v2` and `muse_permission`, each with a `.events` golden, and transcripts written
in the tests. The test binary is the fake `muse` too: it replays with T3's rules (writes may come out
of order within a turn, never past the next one; `<any>`; the ids roll mints bound to the recorded
ones and rewritten in what it sends back; request ids rebound; T3's `<runtime_info>` part dropped),
records its arguments, directory and environment, and fails loudly (exit 3) on a frame it didn't
expect or a transcript not played to its end.

| Feature | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| A session in full access: `serve --trust-workspace --disable-sandbox`, `initialize` (schema 1, session MCP), `session/start` with roll's minted ids, the real workspace path, the model and effort; streamed text; housekeeping items (the user's message, reminder children) hidden; usage and the context window | `agent-host/tests/muse.rs::simple`, `agent-host/src/provider/muse/rpc.rs::minted_ids_are_uuid_v7` |
| Approvals in `ask`: `promptUnmatched`/`:ask-me`, the server request answered `{}`, Allow once and Reject (Muse's `abort`) as accept and decline; the narrowest choice per decision; withdrawn when Muse settles them or the turn ends; an unknown server request refused | `agent-host/tests/muse.rs::muse_permission`, `agent-host/tests/muse.rs::unanswered_requests_are_withdrawn`, `agent-host/src/provider/muse/translate.rs::approval_choices_are_the_narrowest` |
| Questions: headers, options and multi-select; answers as labels with the rest as free text (500 characters at most) | `agent-host/tests/muse.rs::questions_are_answered`, `agent-host/src/provider/muse/translate.rs::answers_take_labels_and_free_text` |
| Stop: `turn/interrupt` for the turn (also before Muse started it), a turn joined to ours stopped first; a cancelled tool | `agent-host/tests/muse.rs::turn_interrupt`, `agent-host/tests/muse.rs::a_turn_muse_starts_while_ours_runs_is_joined` |
| Steering (`turn/steer` with the turn's id), roll's instructions as the first text part, images inline, the effort | `agent-host/tests/muse.rs::steering_and_instructions` |
| `/compact` → `session/compact` with the turn's id; the compaction item ends the turn; `noop` fails it | `agent-host/tests/muse.rs::compaction` |
| Subagents with their role, prompt and result, their child session's items beneath them and settled with them; a subagent's approval on the subagent; in full access a child's approval approved for it; one between turns asked with the next turn | `agent-host/tests/muse.rs::subagent_v2`, `agent-host/tests/muse.rs::full_access_approves_a_childs_approval`, `agent-host/tests/muse.rs::a_childs_approval_between_turns_waits_for_the_next` |

### The ACP registry (`agent-host/tests/registry.rs`, `agent-host/src/registry/*` unit tests)

A local HTTP server plays the registry (`[registry] url`) and serves a `.tar.gz` and a `.zip`
holding a tiny ACP agent that answers `initialize`.

| Feature | Tests |
| --- | --- |
| `registry.list`: the index parsed with T3's bounds (512 agents, 1 MiB, ids, versions without `.`/`..`, HTTPS URLs without credentials, exact package versions), bad entries dropped and the rest kept; kinds for this platform, installable, integrity, installed and offered (a known agent under roll's own name: `github-copilot-cli` → `acp:copilot`) | `agent-host/tests/registry.rs::lists_installs_and_removes_an_agent_without_a_restart`, `agent-host/src/registry/index.rs::drops_entries_that_break_a_bound_and_keeps_the_rest`, `agent-host/src/registry/index.rs::envelope_bounds`, `agent-host/src/registry/index.rs::exact_package_versions`, `agent-host/src/registry.rs::registry_ids_map_onto_providers_roll_already_has` |
| an agent installed from the registry stands for its known name while that one isn't installed (`grok-build` and `acp:grok`): the machine lists it once, in `hello` and its settings | `agent-host/tests/registry.rs::an_installed_agent_stands_for_the_known_one_it_is` |
| The list reused for 5 minutes, an install always fetches; offline it falls back to the copy kept at `<home>/acp/registry.json` and says why; with no copy, empty with why | `agent-host/tests/registry.rs::the_list_is_reused_for_a_while_and_kept_for_when_the_registry_is_down` |
| Target selection: binary for this OS and arch (Windows on ARM takes x86-64), then npx, then uvx; `cmd` normalised (no absolute path, drive or `..`) | `agent-host/src/registry/index.rs::choices_go_binary_then_npx_then_uvx`, `agent-host/src/registry/index.rs::commands_are_relative_paths_inside_the_archive` |
| `registry.install`: downloaded, checked, unpacked in Rust into `<home>/acp/tools/<id>/<version>/<target>/`, `installed.json` written, the provider `acp:<id>` offered and probed without a restart (named as the registry names it), offered again by the next start; installing it again only offers it; an agent the machine has already is refused (conflict); unknown agents not found | `agent-host/tests/registry.rs::lists_installs_and_removes_an_agent_without_a_restart`, `agent-host/src/registry.rs::installed_json_round_trips_and_drops_what_doesnt_hold_up` |
| Archives: `.tar.gz`, `.tar.bz2`, `.zip` (deflate) and a bare executable; symlinks, hardlinks, devices, FIFOs, absolute paths, `..` and duplicates refused; entries and unpacked bytes capped; the command must be a regular file inside the folder | `agent-host/src/registry/archive.rs::unpacks_a_tar_gz_and_finds_the_command`, `agent-host/src/registry/archive.rs::refuses_unsafe_tar_entries`, `agent-host/src/registry/archive.rs::caps_entries_and_unpacked_bytes`, `agent-host/src/registry/archive.rs::unpacks_a_zip_and_refuses_unsafe_entries` |
| A SHA-256 mismatch (or a failed download) fails and leaves nothing: no files, no entry, no provider | `agent-host/tests/registry.rs::a_bad_checksum_fails_and_leaves_nothing` |
| Packages with the machine's own npm/uv into the daemon's folder: which npm command runs, uv's command names | `agent-host/src/registry/packages.rs::npm_commands` |
| Registry variables that could hold a credential are never used (parsed out; dropped from `installed.json`) | `agent-host/src/registry/index.rs::parses_an_entry`, `agent-host/tests/registry.rs::lists_installs_and_removes_an_agent_without_a_restart`, `agent-host/src/registry.rs::installed_json_round_trips_and_drops_what_doesnt_hold_up` |
| `registry.remove`: provider, entry and files gone; not installed from the registry → not found | `agent-host/tests/registry.rs::lists_installs_and_removes_an_agent_without_a_restart` |

## Measurements

| What | Test | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| landing throughput: 40,000 changes, 64 agents, hot-file overlap (release; `pnpm test:code --stress` runs it) | `code-store/tests/throughput.rs::land_40k_merges` | 2026-10-22, 16-core host shared with other builds (load 6–11): tmpfs 883–911 merges/s, real disk (SATA SSD RAID 0) 448–496 merges/s, with every landing fsynced before it's acknowledged (architecture.md, "Landing") |
| first codebase-index build of a 50,000-file repo, streamed and committed 200 files at a time (release, `CODE_INDEX_SCALE_N=50000`, run under a 2 GB memory limit) | `index_scale.rs::a_big_first_build_is_streamed_and_bounded` | 120 s (417 files/s) on real disk; peak RSS grew 51 MB (207 → 258 MB); 500,000 symbols, 12.4 M trigram postings. `pnpm test:code` runs 600 files |
| 2,000 changes through the merge queue with 2% culprits (`CODE_QUEUE_N`) | `queue.rs::throughput_with_culprits` | every other change lands once, in order, on a linear main |

## Not covered by automated tests

- **Timing that's a fixed constant**: the GitHub webhook's 202 after its 8 s budget, the queue
  worker retiring after 300 s idle, MCP's 15 s keep-alive comments, `roll code watch`'s 10 s rate
  line, and the review-app/webhook delivery backoff schedule (×3 up to 6 h; the 8 attempts,
  `failed` and redelivery are tested). Testing them would mean waiting that long.
- **Faults that need injection**: 500s, the `review` blocker ("couldn't check reviews"), the queue's
  "couldn't be rebased" ejection, a store failure mid-rebase, the reviewer's `posting` stage
  (microseconds long; checked as an allowed value).
- **Push-ingest corner cases from GitHub**: a gitlink in GitHub's changes, roll's main moving on all
  3 merge retries, a conflict while landing GitHub's push. Mirror RemoteAhead/Diverged pauses are
  covered by the scheduler tests (`code-github/tests/mirror.rs`), not end to end.
- **Large-scale limits**: MCP's 10,000-session cap; JSON bodies over 64 MiB answer 400, not 413.
- **Old-side threads on a change with no merge base** (422): the API can't build such a change.
- **The dashboard's README rendering, sign-in dialog, board and app shell**: README HTML is
  sanitized on the server (`code-control/src/render.rs` tests); the rest are type-checked and were
  driven in headless Chrome in the integration pass. Component tests cover the diff, change page,
  markdown, queue tab, reviewer header, blame gutter and secrets shown once.
- **`roll code delete`'s prompt on a real terminal** (needs a pty; the unattended refusal is
  tested), colour output, and `create --github` success (needs a GitHub App; the GitHub tests cover
  connecting through the API).
- **S3-backed storage for code.** Tests use the local object store, the same `pg_storage::Objects`
  API the other suites test against S3. The local store doesn't fsync blobs.
- **The desktop app's Electron wiring** (`apps/desktop/src/main/main.ts`: the two windows, tray, badge,
  notifications, IPC sender checks, single-instance and `open-url` routing, quit stopping the
  daemon). It's type-checked and its decisions are the tested modules above, but a window can't
  start on the dev server (no GTK; Xvfb alone isn't enough). Packaging is checked by building the
  Linux AppImage and deb (`pnpm --filter @roll/desktop dist --linux`).
- **The CLI release workflow** (`.github/workflows/release-cli.yml`) runs only on a `cli-v*` tag;
  its builds are smoke-tested in the workflow itself (`roll --version`, `roll agent --help`), and
  the install script is tested against a local release by `pnpm test:agents`.
- **Share pages in a browser**: the replay is tested in jsdom (`share.test.tsx`) and the page and
  preview image over HTTP (`pnpm test:agents`); how it looks was checked by hand.
